Method: SG-SRL通过参考自由强化学习，利用跨语言语义奖励模型（由跨语言重排序器实例化）在源语言数据上训练，随后通过轻量级恢复阶段（使用小规模平行语料）恢复流畅性和简洁性。

Result: 在中文到泰语生成任务上，SG-SRL相比冷启动SFT提升了语义基础和事实覆盖率。

Conclusion: 长形式迁移和基于藏语嵌入的奖励分析表明，SG-SRL具有良好泛化性，且基于编码器的语义奖励可在真实低资源语言环境中替代基于LLM的重排序器。

Abstract: Low-resource target-language generation is often limited by scarce parallel data, while high-resource source-language monolingual data is abundant but difficult to use with standard supervised fine-tuning. We propose Source-Grounded Semantic Reinforcement Learning (SG-SRL), a resource-utilization framework that converts source-language monolingual data into cross-lingual semantic supervision for target-language generation. SG-SRL performs reference-free reinforcement learning (RL) on source-language data using a cross-lingual semantic reward model, instantiated by a cross-lingual reranker that scores the semantic relevance between the source input and the target-language generation. While this induces severe verbosity-based reward hacking, a lightweight recovery stage using a small parallel corpus restores fluency, conciseness, and task format while preserving the semantic gains. Experiments on Chinese-to-Thai generation show that SG-SRL improves semantic grounding and factual coverage over cold-start SFT. Additional analyses on long-form transfer and Tibetan embedding-based rewards clarify the generalization behavior of SG-SRL and show that an encoder-based semantic reward can substitute for an LLM-based reranker in a realistic low-resource language setting.

</details>


### [40] [From Blind Guess to Informed Judgment: Teaching LLMs to Evaluate Materials by Building Knowledge-Augmented Preference Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29555)
*Yeyong Yu,Wenya Hu,Xing Wu,Quan Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MaterEval通过知识增强的偏好信号框架，为每个材料候选自动生成两种评估（专家规则引导的判断和盲测），通过偏好数据训练通用LLM，使其具备可靠的材料评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着候选生成和高通量实验的推进，材料发现的主要瓶颈已从性质预测转向在大量候选集中做出可靠评估。通用LLM缺乏材料特定的评估标准，无法可靠评估材料候选。

Method: 提出知识增强的偏好信号框架MaterEval：1）为每个候选自动生成两种评估（遵循专家规则的引导判断和移除规则的盲测）；2）将两种评估配对为偏好数据，训练通用LLM从直觉判断转向可靠评估；3）引入快慢推理方案，将大规模快速筛选与深度审查解耦；4）以高熵合金评估为案例研究。

Result: 小型开源LLM在无需外部检索的情况下，在准确率、结论一致性和证据区分度上取得显著提升，接近基于规则的闭源LLM性能。

Conclusion: 专家规则可系统转化为可学习的偏好信号，实现低成本、可部署的材料评估模块，用于自主材料发现循环。

Abstract: As candidate generation and high-throughput experimentation advance, the primary bottleneck in materials discovery is shifting from property prediction to making reliable evaluations among massive candidate sets. We propose a Knowledge-Augmented Preference Signals Framework, MaterEval, that automatically produces, for the same candidate, two evaluations: an informed judgment that follows expert rules and provides supporting evidence, and a rule-removed blind guess. By pairing the two evaluations as preference data, we guide general-purpose large language models (LLMs), originally lacking materials-specific criteria, from intuitive judgment toward reliable evaluation supported by explicit evidence. To balance throughput, cost, and reliability, we further introduce a fast-slow reasoning scheme that decouples large-scale rapid screening from in-depth review on a small subset. Using high-entropy alloy (HEA) assessment as a case study, we show that, without external retrieval and relying solely on internalized capabilities, small open-source LLMs achieve substantial gains in accuracy, conclusion consistency, and evidence discrimination, approaching the performance of rule-based closed-source LLMs. These results demonstrate that expert rules can be systematically transformed into learnable preference signals, enabling a low-cost and deployable evaluation module for autonomous materials discovery loops.

</details>


### [41] [LiteCoder-Terminal: Scaling Long-Horizon Terminal Environments for Learning Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29559)
*Xiaoxuan Peng,Kaiqi Zhang,Xinyu Lu,Boxi Cao,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 LiteCoder-Terminal-Gen，一种零依赖合成管道，可从领域规范自动生成可执行且可验证的终端训练环境。作者构建了两个大规模资源：包含 11,255 条专家轨迹的 LiteCoder-Terminal-SFT 数据集和包含 602 个可验证环境的 LiteCoder-Terminal-RL 数据集。在 SFT 数据集上对 Qwen 系列模型进行监督微调后，32B 变体在 Terminal Bench 1.0、2.0 和 Pro 上分别达到 29.06%、18.54% 和 34.00% 的 pass@1 准确率。在 RL 环境上应用直接多轮偏好优化（DMPO）可获得额外性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端环境中的语言智能体训练目前受限于对爬取外部仓库的依赖，这限制了领域多样性、环境可控性和对特定能力缺陷的针对性训练。

Method: 提出 LiteCoder-Terminal-Gen 零依赖合成管道，可直接从领域规范自动生成可执行且可验证的终端训练环境。构建了两个大规模资源：LiteCoder-Terminal-SFT（11,255 条专家轨迹，覆盖 10 个领域）和 LiteCoder-Terminal-RL（602 个可验证环境用于轨迹级偏好优化）。

Result: 在 SFT 数据集上对 Qwen 系列模型进行监督微调后，智能体显著优于基线模型。32B 变体在 Terminal Bench 1.0、2.0 和 Pro 上分别取得 29.06%、18.54% 和 34.00% 的 pass@1 准确率。在 RL 环境上应用 DMPO 进一步提升了性能。

Conclusion: 完全合成的、可执行的终端环境为掌握复杂的现实世界命令行工作流程提供了可扩展且可验证的监督信号。

Abstract: Mastering terminal environments requires language agents capable of multi-step planning, feedback-grounded execution, and dynamic state adaptation. However, training such agents is currently bottlenecked by a reliance on scraped external repositories, which limits domain diversity, environment controllability, and the targeting of specific capability deficits. We introduce LiteCoder-Terminal-Gen, a zero-dependency synthesis pipeline that autonomously generates executable and verifiable terminal training environments directly from domain specifications. Using this framework, we construct two large-scale resources: LiteCoder-Terminal-SFT, comprising 11,255 expert trajectories across 10 domains, and LiteCoder-Terminal-RL, featuring 602 verifiable environments for trajectory-level preference optimization. Supervised fine-tuning of Qwen-family models on our SFT dataset yields agents that significantly outperform their base counterparts. Notably, our 32B variant achieves 29.06%, 18.54%, and 34.00% pass@1 on Terminal Bench 1.0, 2.0, and Pro, respectively. Furthermore, applying Direct Multi-turn Preference Optimization (DMPO) on our RL environments yields additional performance gains. These results systematically demonstrate that fully synthetic, executable environments offer a scalable and verifiable supervision signal for mastering complex, real-world command-line workflows.

</details>


### [42] [GAPD: Gold-Action Policy Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning in Knowledge Base Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29584)
*Xin Sun,Jianan Xie,Zhongqi Chen,Qiang Liu,Shu Wu,Bowen Song,Weiqiang Wang,Zilei Wang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GAPD框架，通过将黄金动作策略蒸馏到强化学习的学生策略中，为KBQA任务提供密集的token级指导，解决了当前RL方法中中间动作监督不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习是KBQA的自然选择，但当前RL方法主要优化稀疏的最终答案奖励，导致中间动作错误缺乏强监督。在逻辑形式标注的KBQA基准测试中，尽管黄金逻辑形式可以转换为可执行的动作序列，但现有管道主要将其用于热启动数据构建，而非在线策略RL更新。

Method: GAPD在基于结果的RL中添加密集的token级指导。通过MID-ANCHOR MATCHING对齐黄金动作与学生策略的rollouts：将学生探索期间和黄金执行期间达到的中间实体视为状态锚点，通过这些探索的实体集匹配学生状态与黄金状态。当前策略（条件于对齐的黄金动作）作为stop-gradient教师，其token分布被蒸馏回普通学生策略。

Result: GAPD在WebQSP、GrailQA和GraphQ基准测试上持续超越当前最先进水平。

Conclusion: 提出的框架成功改进了RL-based KBQA系统，通过提供更好的中间动作监督，在三个主流KBQA基准测试上实现了SOTA性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is a natural fit for agentic knowledge base question answering (KBQA), where a model must issue executable actions, observe knowledge-base feedback, and eventually return an answer. However, current RL-based KBQA systems mainly optimize sparse rewards from the final answer, leaving intermediate action errors weakly supervised. This is especially limiting for logical-form annotated KBQA benchmarks: gold logical forms can be converted into executable action sequences, but existing pipelines use them mainly for warm-start data construction rather than for on-policy RL updates. We propose GAPD, a training-time Gold-Action Policy Distillation framework that adds dense token-level guidance to outcome-based RL. To align gold actions with on-policy student rollouts, GAPD uses MID-ANCHOR MATCHING: it treats the intermediate entities reached during student exploration and gold execution as state anchors, and matches student states to gold states through these explored entity sets. The current policy conditioned on this aligned gold action serves as a stop-gradient teacher, whose token distribution is distilled back to the ordinary student policy over generated action-token spans. GAPD consistently surpasses the current state of the art on WebQSP, GrailQA, and GraphQ.

</details>


### [43] [Verifiable Rewards Beyond Math and Code: Lightweight Corpus-Grounded Process Supervision for Factual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29648)
*Shicheng Fan,Haochang Hao,Dehai Min,Weihao Liu,Philip S. Yu,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CorVer，一种基于维基百科共现统计的轻量级句子级过程奖励，用于强化学习中的事实准确性提升，替代昂贵的神经验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习提升事实准确性面临奖励设计困境：响应级奖励过于粗糙，无法区分推理轨迹中的正确与错误陈述；句子级替代方案（如NLI验证器、LLM裁判、知识验证管道）在RL规模部署成本高且对稀有实体事实不可靠。

Method: CorVer采用基于维基百科共现统计的语料库 grounded 信号，通过简单对齐将句子级奖励映射到token级优势值，仅需0.5B提取器和每句一次语料库查询。

Result: 在30个（模型、基准）单元（6个指令微调模型：3B-14B，5个QA基准）上，所有单元均优于原始基线；平均TriviaQA提升4.1个百分点；在20个可行配置中18个优于4个神经验证器基线；训练速度快4.8-8.4倍。

Conclusion: CorVer提供了一种高效、有效的神经验证器替代方案，显著提升知识密集型QA的事实准确性，同时大幅降低计算成本。

Abstract: Applying reinforcement learning to improve factual accuracy in knowledge-intensive question answering faces a reward design dilemma. Response-level rewards provide only coarse supervision and cannot distinguish correct from incorrect statements within a reasoning trace. Sentence-level alternatives offer finer-grained feedback, but typically rely on NLI verifiers, LLM judges, or knowledge-verification pipelines that are expensive to deploy at RL scale and often unreliable for rare-entity facts, where accurate reward signals are especially important. We propose CorVer (Corpus Verify), a lightweight, plug-in-ready process reward that replaces neural verifiers with a corpus-grounded signal derived from Wikipedia co-occurrence statistics. CorVer assigns sentence-level credit and maps it to token-level advantages via a simple alignment, requiring only a 0.5B extractor and a single corpus lookup per sentence. Across 30 (model, benchmark) cells spanning six instruction-tuned models (3B to 14B) and five QA benchmarks, CorVer improves over the raw baseline for every cell, with an average TriviaQA gain of +4.1 pp. It also outperforms four neural-verifier baselines in 18 of 20 cells under their feasible configurations, while training 4.8 to 8.4x faster.

</details>


### [44] [Spurious Prompts: Can Irrelevant Prompts Steer Large Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29678)
*Pawel Batorski,Abtin Pourhadi,Jerzy Sarosiek,Przemyslaw Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究提示词无关性对大语言模型行为的影响，发现“虚假提示词”可以通过黑盒搜索发现，能有效引导模型表现或改变其行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注任务相关的指令、演示或推理提示，本文探索一种不同的提示敏感性：语义上与任务无关的提示是否能引导模型行为。

Method: 提出一种简单的黑盒搜索过程来发现虚假提示词（spurious prompts）。

Result: 在推理和问答基准测试中，从0.8B到27B参数、涵盖三个模型家族的模型上，虚假提示词能提升性能，常达到或超过标准提示基线和任务感知提示优化；同时也能引导模型产生意外行为（如反复选择第一个答案选项、输出错误答案、生成特定数字等）。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可以被系统性地引导，即使提示与任务无关，揭示了提示敏感性的新维度。

Abstract: Large language models are highly sensitive to prompts, but this sensitivity is usually studied through task-relevant instructions, demonstrations, or reasoning cues. In this paper, we study a different form of prompt sensitivity: whether prompts that are semantically unrelated to the task can nevertheless steer model behavior. We call them spurious prompts and show their surprising efficacy. We also propose a simple black-box search procedure for discovering them. Across reasoning and question-answering benchmarks, using models ranging from 0.8B to 27B parameters and spanning three model families, we show that spurious prompts can improve performance, often matching or outperforming standard prompting baselines and task-aware prompt optimization. We further show that they can steer models toward unintended behaviors, such as repeatedly selecting the first answer option, producing incorrect answers, returning an even, prime or small number without explicitly instructing the model to do so. These findings reveal a new kind of prompt sensitivity: LLMs can be systematically steered by prompts that are unrelated to the task they are asked to solve. Our code is available at https://github.com/Batorskq/spurious

</details>


### [45] [Teaching Language Models to Check Grounded Claim Factuality with Human Test-Taking Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29712)
*Yuxuan Ye,Raul Santos-Rodriguez,Edwin Simpson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于明确测试策略的文本真实性检查新方法，将问题转化为真假阅读理解任务，并训练小型语言模型替代大型模型以降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation:  grounded claim factuality checking 对 RAG 等 LLM 应用很重要。现有方法存在局限：基于蕴含分类器的指标需要数据集特定的阈值调优，而基于 LLM 的方法通常使用直接提示，未能充分利用 LLM 的推理能力。

Method:  将 grounded claim factuality checking 形式化为真假阅读理解任务，通过明确的测试策略提示 LLM 进行高效推理；随后采用监督微调 (SFT) 和自我修正机制训练小型语言模型 (SLM) 替代 LLM，实现低成本推理。

Result:  相比无引导的开放推理，该方法减少 80% 以上的 token 使用量；在两个事实性基准测试中达到与更昂贵的替代方案相当的性能，并在其中一个基准上取得新的 SOTA；训练后的 SLM 性能与强基线相当，同时兼具低推理成本和生成支持推理的解释性。

Conclusion:  该方法有效降低了推理成本，同时保持了竞争性能，训练得到的 SLM 可作为 LLM 的低成本替代方案用于 grounded claim factuality checking。

Abstract: Grounded claim factuality checking is important for large language model (LLM) applications such as retrieval-augmented generation, as it helps users assess the correctness of generated outputs. Existing metrics using entailment classifiers require dataset-specific threshold tuning, while LLM-based approaches often use direct prompting, which underutilises the reasoning capabilities of LLMs. We address this by formulating grounded claim factuality checking as a true/false reading comprehension task and prompting LLMs with explicit test-taking strategies for efficient reasoning. Our method reduces token usage by over 80% compared to unguided open-ended reasoning, and achieves competitive performance to more expensive alternatives across two factuality benchmarks, setting a new state of the art on one. To further reduce inference cost, we train small language models (SLMs) to replace LLMs in the checking pipeline. Using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and a self-revision mechanism, the SLMs learn to improve their factuality judgements. Experimental results show that the resulting SLMs perform on par with strong baselines, combining low inference costs with generating supporting rationales to support interpretability. Code and datasets will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [46] [User-Aware Active Knowledge Acquisition for Emotional Support Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29715)
*Mufan Xu,Kehai Chen,Jiahao Hu,Xinchao Xu,Muyun Yang,Tiejun Zhao,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UKA是一种梯度无关的主动对话学习框架，通过显式表示用户需求的不确定性，将主动学习整合到知识获取和响应选择中，利用心智理论的不确定性估计机制来优先响应并获取更 informative 的用户反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感支持在对话系统中的成功取决于适应多轮交互中用户不断演变和隐含的需求。由于用户需求信号通常较弱、间接，需要通过多轮交互才能消除歧义，现有情感支持方法难以高效获取和泛化相关对话知识。

Method: 提出用户感知主动知识获取（UKA）框架，包含：1）梯度无关的主动对话学习；2）显式表示用户需求的不确定性；3）将主动学习整合到知识获取和响应选择；4）提出心智理论不确定性估计机制，用于优先响应并激发更informative的用户反馈。

Result: 在多个对话基准和模型架构上的实验表明，UKA在对话质量和用户对齐方面始终优于强基线，能够高效探索用户对齐的对话知识，同时保持测试时的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: UKA能够在训练期间高效探索用户对齐的对话知识，同时保持测试时的鲁棒性，为提升对话系统的情感支持能力提供了一种有效的方法。

Abstract: Emotional support plays an important role in dialogue systems, and its success depends on adapting to a user's evolving and implicit needs across multi-turn interactions while leveraging the strong reasoning capacity of large language models. However, since signals about user needs are often weak, indirect, and can only be disambiguated through multi-turn interaction, existing emotional support methods often struggle to acquire and generalize relevant conversational knowledge efficiently. To bridge this gap, we introduce User-Aware Active Knowledge Acquisition (UKA), a gradient-free active dialogue learning framework that explicitly represents uncertainty about user needs and incorporates active learning into both knowledge acquisition and response selection.We propose a Theory-of-Mind uncertainty estimation mechanism that allows the model to prioritize responses, thereby eliciting more informative user feedback. UKA is capable of efficiently exploring user-aligned conversational knowledge during training while maintaining robustness at test time. Experiments across multiple dialogue benchmarks and model architectures demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms strong baselines in dialogue quality and user alignment.

</details>


### [47] [HTAM: Hierarchical Transition-Attended Memory for Operator Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29734)
*Yining Zhang,Mingyang Yi,Chen Wang,Xuwen Xiang,Tianhe Jia,Zedong Dan,Chengqing Zong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HTAM是一种用于LLM-based GPU操作符优化的粗到细框架，通过构建分层转移图来组织优化经验，在KernelBench上显著提升正确性、快速解决方案率和加速比


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能GPU内核对LLM部署至关重要，但优化仍需专业知识。现有LLM方法面临粒度不匹配：粗略提示可重用但难执行，详细记忆可操作但扩大搜索空间。关键挑战是以适当粒度组织优化经验。

Method: 提出HTAM（分层转移注意力内存），构建两级分层转移图（HTG）组织粗粒度全局方向、详细本地策略和步骤间转移经验。在每个进化步骤，从当前状态和优化历史选择全局方向，检索相应本地策略内存，指导CUDA代码生成。

Result: 在KernelBench套件上，HTAM在正确性、快速解决方案率和加速比方面持续优于LLM基线。后端和Robust-KBench研究显示结构化内存具有可迁移收益。

Conclusion: HTAM成功解决了LLM-based GPU操作符优化中的粒度不匹配问题，通过分层组织优化经验实现了性能提升，证明了结构化记忆在硬件感知搜索中的有效性。

Abstract: High-performance GPU kernels are essential for efficient LLM deployment, yet optimizing them remains expertise-intensive. Recent LLM-based code generation makes automatic GPU operator generation promising, but operator optimization remains a hardware-aware search problem. Existing LLM-based methods face a granularity mismatch: coarse hints are reusable but hard to execute, whereas detailed memories are actionable but enlarge the search space and obscure optimization bottlenecks. The key challenge is therefore to organize optimization experience at an appropriate granularity. To address this issue, this paper proposes HTAM (Hierarchical Transition-Attended Memory), a coarse-to-fine framework for LLM-based operator optimization. HTAM builds a two-level Hierarchical Transition Graph (HTG) to organize coarse global directions, detailed local strategies, and transition experience between optimization steps. During each evolution step, HTAM selects a global direction from the current state and recent optimization history, retrieves the corresponding local strategy memory, and uses it to guide concrete CUDA code generation. Experiments on the full KernelBench suite demonstrate that HTAM consistently improves correctness, fast-solution rate, and speedup over LLM-based baselines, while backend and Robust-KBench studies indicate transferable benefits from structured memory.

</details>


### [48] [Multi-Legal-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Legal Reasoning Across Jurisdictions, Languages, and Legal Traditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29738)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Multi-Legal-Bench是首个跨司法管辖区法律基准，评估7个前沿LLM在6个国家、4种语言家族和1.34亿判决上的表现。研究发现任务相关的少样本效应在所有司法管辖区重复出现，无单一模型主导任何语言排名，跨语言少样本迁移不遵循语言接近性，标记集对齐比语言家族更能预测迁移质量，且标记器生育力不显著预测跨语言准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律NLP基准 overwhelmingly 评估单一语言或聚合任务，这些任务在不同司法管辖区根本不同，使得跨语言比较不可能。

Method: 引入Multi-Legal-Bench基准，定义5个任务（法院类型分类、判决形式分类、案件结果预测、法律规范提取、原因类别预测），映射到6个国家（乌克兰、法国、荷兰、波兰、捷克、立陶宛）、4种语言家族和1.34亿判决的元数据。评估7个前沿LLM在AWS Bedrock上的零样本和3少样本提示，以及4个小型/中型模型（3-12B）用于扩展分析。

Result: 1) 乌克兰中发现的任务相关少样本效应在所有司法管辖区重复；2) 无单一模型主导任何语言排名；3) 跨语言少样本迁移不遵循语言接近性（UA→FR比UA→PL更好）；4) 标记集对齐比语言家族更好地预测迁移质量；5) 标记器生育力（2.3倍差异）不显著预测跨语言准确率（r=-0.27, p=0.14）。

Conclusion: 模型架构和预训练数据主导标记器效率，跨司法管辖区评估对法律NLP中的公平跨语言比较至关重要。

Abstract: Legal NLP benchmarks overwhelmingly evaluate a single language or aggregate tasks that differ fundamentally across jurisdictions, making cross-lingual comparison impossible. We introduce Multi-Legal-Bench, the first cross-jurisdictional legal benchmark that evaluates identical tasks across six countries (Ukraine, France, Netherlands, Poland, Czech Republic, Lithuania), four language families, and 134 million court decisions. The benchmark defines five tasks court-type classification, judgment form classification, case-outcome prediction, legal norm extraction, and cause category prediction mapped to structured metadata from national court registries, forming a deliberately sparse 5x6 task-jurisdiction matrix (20 of 30 cells filled). We evaluate 7 frontier LLMs under zero-shot and 3-shot prompting via AWS Bedrock, with 4 additional small/medium models (3-12B) for scaling analysis. Our results reveal that: (1) task-dependent few-shot effects discovered in Ukrainian replicate across all jurisdictions; (2) no single model dominates any language rankings shift with both task and jurisdiction; (3) cross-lingual few-shot transfer does not follow language proximity: UA->FR (Romance, -2.1 pp) transfers better than UA->PL (Slavic, -13.7 pp), with label-set alignment predicting transfer quality better than language family; and (4) tokenizer fertility, despite a 2.3x spread, does not significantly predict cross-lingual accuracy (r=-0.27, p=0.14), suggesting that model architecture and pretraining data dominate tokenizer efficiency. We release all data, prompts, and model predictions.

</details>


### [49] [ActTraitBench: Quantifying the Knowledge-Decision Gap in Large Language Models via Human-Grounded Behavioral Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29791)
*Yutong Yang,Chenxi Miao,Weikang Li,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ActTraitBench框架和CoCA方法，量化LLM的知识-决策差距，发现大模型行为一致性更低，CoCA能有效提升推理模型对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在显式自我报告上能模拟人格，但在隐性行为决策中常偏离，现有基准缺乏构念效度、维度纠缠和分布偏差，难以准确测量这一不对称性。

Method: 基于人类实证数据，建立心理测量维度与行为范式的映射，通过分位数映射进行分布校准，提出CoCA推理时干预模块。

Result: 14个主流LLM均存在知识-决策差距，更大更强的模型行为偏离更显著；CoCA在推理模型上提升对齐，在小模型上暴露能力局限。

Conclusion: 首次系统量化LLM人格一致性不对称，提供可插拔的对齐方法，为模型评估与对齐研究提供新基准与干预手段。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) can convincingly simulate personas in explicit self-reports, they often deviate in implicit behavioral decisions, revealing a substantial Knowledge-Decision Gap ($G_{\text{KD}}$). Existing benchmarks struggle to measure this asymmetry due to limited construct validity, multi-dimensional entanglement, and distributional biases in LLM-based evaluation. To address these issues, we propose ActTraitBench, a human-grounded evaluation framework for measuring personality consistency in LLMs. Grounded in empirical human data, ActTraitBench establishes one-to-one mappings between psychometric facets and behavioral paradigms, and applies a Distributional Calibration via Quantile Mapping procedure to align LLM-judge score distributions with human norms. Experiments on 14 mainstream LLMs reveal a pervasive knowledge-decision asymmetry, where larger and more capable models often exhibit stronger behavioral divergence despite highly consistent self-reports. To mitigate this gap, we further introduce the Chain of Cognitive Alignment (CoCA), a plug-and-play inference-time intervention that improves alignment in reasoning-capable frontier models while exposing clear capability limitations in smaller architectures.

</details>


### [50] [Nine Judges, Two Effective Votes: Correlated Errors Undermine LLM Evaluation Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29800)
*Guneet Kohli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM-as-a-judge panels aggregate votes from multiple models, but this study finds that the 9 frontier LLMs on a panel provide only about 2 independent votes' worth of information, losing three-quarters of their nominal independence because models make the same mistakes. The panel's accuracy falls 8-22 percentage points short of independent voting, and even the best single judge outperforms the full panel. Neither adding more judges nor using smarter aggregation algorithms helps, indicating that scaling up panels cannot substitute for genuinely independent evaluation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To measure the true informational value of LLM-as-a-judge panels and quantify how far their reliability falls short of the independent-voting ideal.

Method: Developed a framework to measure the true informational value of panels using the Kish effective sample size (n_eff) and a Condorcet null model. Tested a panel of 9 frontier LLMs from 7 model families on three natural language inference datasets (each with 100 human annotations per item), evaluating across prompt variants, temperatures, chain-of-thought reasoning, and a pairwise preference task (RewardBench).

Result: The 9 judges effectively provide only about 2 independent votes' worth of information. Roughly three-quarters of the panel's nominal independence is lost because models make the same mistakes on the same items. The panel's actual accuracy falls 8-22 percentage points short of what independent voting would achieve, and the best single judge matches or outperforms the full panel across all conditions. Established methods close at most 11% of this gap, even with access to the correct answers.

Conclusion: The bottleneck is correlated judges, not the aggregation algorithm, implying that scaling up panels cannot substitute for genuinely independent evaluation. Neither adding more judges nor using smarter aggregation algorithms helps mitigate the loss of independence.

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge panels aggregate votes from multiple models, with the expectation that diverse models yield more reliable evaluations. We develop a framework to measure the true informational value of such panels and quantify how far their reliability falls short of the independent-voting ideal. Testing a panel of 9 frontier LLMs from 7 model families on three natural language inference datasets (each with 100 human annotations per item), we find that the 9 judges effectively provide only about 2 independent votes' worth of information. Roughly three-quarters of the panel's nominal independence is lost because the models make the same mistakes on the same items. The consequences are stark: the panel's actual accuracy falls 8-22 percentage points short of what independent voting would achieve, and the best single judge matches or outperforms the full panel across all conditions. Neither adding more judges nor using smarter aggregation algorithms helps -- established methods close at most 11% of this gap, even with access to the correct answers. We quantify these findings using the Kish effective sample size (n_eff) and a Condorcet null model, and show the deficit is robust across prompt variants, temperatures, chain-of-thought reasoning, and a pairwise preference task (RewardBench). The bottleneck is correlated judges, not the aggregation algorithm, implying that scaling up panels cannot substitute for genuinely independent evaluation.

</details>


### [51] [Towards Localized and Disentangled Knowledge Editing for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29826)
*Leijiang Gu,Zhen Zeng,Feng Li,Xinjian Gao,Zenglin Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种新的框架LDKE，通过定位特定模型层和分离目标相关输入与无关输入，实现对多模态大语言模型的精确且泛化的知识编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态知识编辑方法在修改目标事实对时，无法泛化到逻辑相关的查询，且常导致对无关但视觉或语义相关的信息产生意外修改。研究者识别并形式化了两个根本失败模式：因果错位（将编辑限制在特定样本）和特征纠缠（导致对耦合但无关信息的意外修改）。

Method: 提出Localized and Disentangled Knowledge Editing (LDKE)框架，包含两个关键组件：Fast Localization模块用于高效识别和更新关键层，以及Disentanglement分类器用于适当路由输入以保留无关知识。

Result: 在多个基准测试和MLLM上的实验表明，LDKE在将编辑传播到相关上下文的同时保持了高局部性，实现了优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: LDKE成功解决了因果错位和特征纠缠问题，实现了多模态知识编辑的精确性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Existing methods in Multimodal Knowledge Editing (MKE) have advanced the ability to correct outdated or inaccurate knowledge in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, they exhibit a critical limitation: while effectively modifying target factual pairs, they fail to generalize edits to logically related queries and often cause unintended alterations to unrelated but visually or semantically linked information. We identify and formalize two underlying failure modes causing this issue: Causal Misalignment, which confines edits to the specific sample, and Feature Entanglement, which causes unintended alterations to coupled but irrelevant information. To address these issues, we propose Localized and Disentangled Knowledge Editing (LDKE), a new framework that achieves precise and generalized editing by localizing fact-specific model layers and disentangling target-relevant inputs from irrelevant ones. Our approach introduces a Fast Localization module to identify and update critical layers efficiently, along with a Disentanglement Classifier that routes inputs appropriately to preserve unrelated knowledge. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks and MLLMs demonstrate that LDKE achieves superior performance in propagating edits to related contexts while maintaining high locality.

</details>


### [52] [EvoRubric: Self-Evolving Rubric-Driven RL for Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29847)
*Xin Guan,Xiaomeng Hu,Shen Huang,Zhenyi Wang,Bo Zhang,Zijian Li,Pengjun Xie,Bo Liu,Jiuxin Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EvoRubric提出一种单策略协同进化强化学习框架，通过动态交替推理与评分标准生成，结合多级验证机制，解决开放性生成任务中奖励信号缺失的问题，在多个领域显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于静态评分标准的RL方法存在策略滞后和依赖昂贵外部模型的局限；开放性生成任务缺乏明确奖励信号，导致LLM对齐效果不佳。

Method: 提出EvoRubric框架：1) 将响应生成与评分标准生成统一为单策略，动态在Reasoner和Rubric Generator间交替；2) 引入多级验证管道（元验证器、零方差剪枝、留一法同行共识）；3) 验证标准动态存入记忆池，生成密集多目标奖励；4) 支持专家先验初始化。

Result: 在医学、写作、科学领域实验显示EvoRubric显著优于传统静态评分和外部LLM驱动方法；初始化专家评分标准后能发现新判别维度，性能更优。

Conclusion: EvoRubric成功消除对静态标准和外部模型的依赖，通过协同进化机制实现动态、可靠的对齐，证明了单策略框架在开放性生成任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has significantly advanced Large Language Models (LLMs) in verifiable domains, but aligning models for open-ended generation remains profoundly challenging due to the lack of definitive rewards. Current rubric-based RL methods mitigate this by employing explicit criteria; however, they rely heavily on static, human-annotated rubrics that inevitably cause policy lag, or expensive external proprietary models for dynamic updates. In this paper, we propose EvoRubric, a novel single-policy co-evolutionary RL framework that eliminates the reliance on static criteria and on external rubric generators. By unifying response generation and rubric generation under a single parameterized policy, EvoRubric dynamically alternates between a Reasoner and a Rubric Generator. To prevent reward hacking and ensure the reliability of generated signals, we introduce a multi-level verification pipeline featuring a meta-verifier, zero-variance pruning, and a Leave-One-Out peer consensus mechanism. Validated criteria are dynamically archived into a memory pool, yielding dense, multi-objective rewards to continuously co-optimize both roles. Extensive experiments across Medical, Writing, and Science domains demonstrate that EvoRubric consistently outperforms traditional static and external-LLM-driven alignment methods. Notably, our framework is compatible with human-expert priors. When initialized with expert-annotated rubrics, EvoRubric can further uncover novel, discriminative dimensions, achieving better performance than relying solely on static expert annotations.

</details>


### [53] [CRITIC-R1: Learning Structured Critics for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29886)
*Wenhan Xiao,Ziwei Zhang,Chuanyue Yu,Xingcheng Fu,Qingyun Sun,Runhua Xu,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CRITIC-R1 是一个基于强化学习的结构化评论框架，将 RAG 评估问题明确化为错误诊断问题，通过分类诊断维度和两种奖励函数设计，有效提高了 RAG 输出的质量和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 方法仍存在幻觉和推理错误问题，虽然近期研究引入外部评论者来优化 RAG 输出，但评论者通常提供粗粒度和结构薄弱的反馈，表现出过度激进的干预，导致嘈杂且不可靠的优化，限制了其纠正效果。

Method: CRITIC-R1 将常见 RAG 错误分类为多个诊断维度（判决、错误位置、推理分析、修复生成），设计两种奖励函数：保守判断对齐（CJA）鼓励校准的高层级判断并缓解过度激进现象，诊断质量对齐（DQA）通过门控奖励进一步提升细粒度诊断反馈。评论者模型使用 GRPO 基础的 RL 训练，从外部 LLM 教师模型收集过程级监督。

Result: 在五个 QA 基准测试中，CRITIC-R1 在强 RAG 基线模型上持续提升了答案质量。

Conclusion: CRITIC-R1 有效解决了现有 RAG 评论方法的局限性，通过结构化诊断和强化学习训练，提供了更可靠且有效的 RAG 输出优化方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves knowledge-intensive question answering by incorporating external evidence. However, existing RAG methods still suffer from hallucinations and subtle reasoning errors. Recent studies introduce external critics to refine RAG outputs, yet they often provide coarse-grained and weakly structured feedback, exhibit over-aggressive intervention, and lead to noisy and unreliable refinement, limiting their effectiveness for correction. To tackle these issues, we propose CRITIC-R1, a structured critic framework that formulates and learns RAG critique as an explicit error diagnosis problem using reinforcement learning (RL). Our framework categorizes common RAG errors into multiple diagnostic dimensions, including verdict, error location, reasoning analysis, and fix generation. To learn these capabilities, we design two reward functions: Conservative Judgement Alignment (CJA) first encourages calibrated high-level judgements while mitigating the over-aggressive phenomenon, whereas Diagnostic Quality Alignment (DQA) further improves fine-grained diagnostic feedback through gated rewards. We train the critic model using GRPO-based RL with process-level supervision collected from external LLM teacher models. Experiments across five QA benchmarks show that CRITIC-R1 consistently improves answer quality over strong RAG baselines.
  Our source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/critic-r1-FCB0

</details>


### [54] [ExCAM: Explainable Cultural Awareness Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29897)
*Christoph Leiter,Haiyue Song,Hour Kaing,Jin Tei,Hideki Tanaka,Masao Utiyama,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ExCAM，首个专门用于识别、评分和解释指令-输出对中文化错误的可解释文化意识度量指标，基于ExCAM40k数据集实现高达80%的错误检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大型语言模型的文化意识对确保生成文本公平性和应用跨文化泛化至关重要。现有基准存在两个主要问题：(1) 创建需要大量时间与成本的人工标注；(2) 缺乏基于自由文本的评估机制，且常依赖过时的评估方法。

Method: ExCAM是一个可解释的文化意识度量指标，通过ExCAM40k数据集进行训练和评估。该数据集由9个现有基准重新格式化并增强合成错误构成。ExCAM能够识别、评分并解释指令-输出对中的文化错误。

Result: 在多个基线模型（包括GPT-5）的对比测试中，ExCAM实现了最高的错误检测率，在平衡测试集上达到80%的准确率。这证明了ExCAM在细粒度和可解释的文化评估方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ExCAM为大型语言模型的文化意识评估开辟了新路径，能够实现自由文本的细粒度、可解释的文化评估，有助于提升模型在不同文化环境中的公平性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Evaluating the cultural awareness of large language models is crucial to ensure the fairness of generated text and the generalizability of applications across the world. Recent benchmarks explore cultural goods like food or values like behavior in stressful situations through the lens of question answering or text generation tasks. However, creating these benchmarks requires time-intensive and costly human annotations. Also, benchmarks that evaluate cultural awareness in free text are scarce and often rely on dated evaluation mechanisms. To address this gap, we introduce ExCAM, an Explainable Cultural Awareness Metric, which is, to our knowledge, the first dedicated evaluation metric that identifies, rates and explains cultural errors in instruction-output pairs. To train and evaluate ExCAM, we introduce ExCAM40k, a dataset comprised of nine existing benchmarks that we reformat and enhance with synthetic errors. Compared to several baselines, including GPT-5, ExCAM achieves the highest error detection rate with up to 80% accuracy on a balanced test set. Therefore, ExCAM opens the pathway towards fine-grained and explainable cultural evaluation of free text.

</details>


### [55] [Does The Way You Plan Matter? An Empirical Study of Planning Representations for LLM Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29927)
*Alejandra Zambrano,Sara Vera Marjanovic,Imene Kerboua,Xing Han Lù,Leila Kosseim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PlanAhead是一个静态规划器-执行器框架，用于评估不同自然语言计划表示（顺序子目标、叙事、伪代码、检查清单）对LLM驱动的web agent性能的影响，发现计划表述和底层LLM都显著影响agent的鲁棒性和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的web agent在有限探索、遗漏关键步骤和对任务约束敏感方面存在困难，先前研究认为这些失败源于规划弱点，但不同自然语言计划表示的影响尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 1. 自动将WebArena任务分为3个难度等级；2. 在"hard"任务上系统评估4种计划表示（顺序子目标、叙事、伪代码、检查清单）；3. 在OpenAI、Alibaba、Google的多模态LLM agent上测试；4. 引入AR和STC两个新指标应对随机性。

Result: 计划表述和底层LLM生成器都显著影响web-agent的鲁棒性和任务成功。不同计划表示在相同LLM和任务上表现差异显著，证明了计划表示方式的重要性。

Conclusion: 为提升web agent性能，需要在规划阶段精心设计计划表示方式，并选择合适的LLM生成器；PlanAhead框架为系统评估计划表示影响提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Despite recent advances, LLM-based web agents still struggle with limited exploration, omission of critical steps, and sensitivity to task constraints. Prior work suggests that many of these failures stem from weaknesses in planning, yet the impact of alternative natural language plan representation remains unexplored. To address this, we introduce PlanAhead, a static planner-executor framework that evaluates the impact of plan representation in agent performance. We first automatically categorize WebArena tasks into 3 difficulty levels, enabling consistent difficulty grading without human annotation. Then we systematically evaluate 4 different plan representations on the tasks categorized as hard: sequential subgoals, narrative, pseudocode, and checklist; across different families of multimodal LLM powered agents (OpenAI, Alibaba, and Google). To account for stochastic variability, we introduce two novel evaluation metrics: Achievement Rate (AR) and Solved-Task Consistency (STC). Our results show that both, the plan formulation and the underlying LLM generating the plan, significantly influence web-agent robustness and task success.

</details>


### [56] [Latent Performance Profiling of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30018)
*Tanmoy Chakraborty,Ayan Sengupta,Suparna Bhattacharya,Partha Pratim Chakrabarti,Amlan Chakrabarti,Supratik Chakraborty,Partha Pratim Das,Lipika Dey,Richa Singh,Mayank Vatsa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Latent Performance Profiling (LPP)框架，从模型隐藏激活和输出分布中提取任务无关的诊断指标，提供超越表面准确率的内在评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准评估存在数据污染、任务范围狭窄、与现实世界可靠性不匹配等问题，仅捕捉输出内容而忽略信息处理方式、不确定性校准和内部知识结构。

Method: 从隐藏激活和输出分布中派生任务无关的诊断；在模型潜在表示和动态上定义标量指标；设计合成探针用于不确定性和符号推理。

Result: 在0.5B-14B的8个LLM上验证，相似基准分数的模型可表现出不同的潜在特征（如熵或适应性差异）；LPP提供稳定的、架构敏感的签名。

Conclusion: 建议报告LPP与基准分数一起，提供更深层、可解释的模型行为理解，支持更可靠的模型选择、安全评估和评估方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently achieve impressive scores on standardized benchmarks, yet accuracy alone offers a limited view of their capabilities. Evaluating open-source LLMs through leaderboards faces persistent issues like data contamination, narrow task scope, and weak alignment with real-world reliability. Benchmark-based evaluations such as MMLU PRO, BBH, or IFEval primarily capture \textit{what} a model outputs on fixed test sets, not \textit{how} it processes information, calibrates uncertainty, or structures internal knowledge. In this article, we advocate for a shift from benchmark-centric evaluation toward a complementary, \textit{state-centered intrinsic assessment} of LLMs. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Latent Performance Profiling (LPP)} -- a framework that derives task-agnostic diagnostics from hidden activations and output distributions. LPP defines a set of scalar metrics on a model's latent representations and dynamics, revealing scale-independent traits that enable interpretable comparisons and uncover hidden vulnerabilities. Unlike static accuracy scores, LPP provides stable, architecture-sensitive signatures across models of similar size. With extensive empirical analyses across eight LLMs, spanning a size range of 0.5B-14B, we demonstrate that models with similar benchmark scores can exhibit contrasting latent profiles, such as differences in entropy or adaptability. Guided by these insights, we design synthetic probes for uncertainty and symbolic reasoning that align with intrinsic metrics while decoupling from leaderboard bias. We recommend that reporting LPP alongside benchmarks provides a deeper, interpretable understanding of model behavior, enabling more reliable model selection, safety assessment, and evaluation beyond surface-level accuracy.

</details>


### [57] [Who Am I? History-Aware Profiles for Student Simulation in Tutoring Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30051)
*Zhangqi Duan,Shuyan Huang,Alexander Scarlatos,Jaewook Lee,Simon Woodhead,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: A key part of developing large language model (LLM)-powered, automated tutoring tools is student simulation, i.e., using LLMs to role-play as students, which can facilitate tutor model evaluation and training. Existing work mostly focuses on within-dialogue simulation, which lacks context on student knowledge and behavior, partly due to not grounding in past student question-answering or dialogue interactions. In this work, we introduce the task of history-conditioned student simulation, where the goal is to accurately predict student dialogue turns by leveraging information in the student's learning history. We propose a two-component framework in which a profile generator summarizes a student's history and a simulator predicts student turns conditioned on the resulting profile. We train both components with reinforcement learning (RL), yielding profiles optimized for faithful student simulation. We evaluate our method and baselines on the first-of-its-kind real-world dataset of student dialogues and question responses that we collect from a math learning platform. Extensive experiments show that our method significantly outperforms baselines, and demonstrate the importance of history, profiles, and RL training.

</details>


### [58] [UniSteer: Text-Guided Flow Matching in Activation Space for Versatile LLM Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30076)
*Yingdong Shi,Ruiming Zhang,Changming Li,Zhiyu Yang,Kaixing Zhang,Jingyi Yu,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniSteer是一种基于文本引导的激活流匹配模型，通过学习自然语言条件下的残差流激活条件分布，实现对大语言模型行为的统一控制，包括行为控制、真实性引导、细粒度概念引导、多约束指令遵循和激活空间分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于激活的控制方法通常依赖固定的引导方向或特定任务的干预模块，难以适应细粒度概念和组合约束，需要为每个目标行为拟合独立的干预机制，缺乏通用性。

Method: UniSteer学习激活空间中的通用条件速度场，从自然语言条件中学习残差流激活的条件分布；推理时通过流逆变换将源激活部分传输到潜在状态，在目标文本条件下重新生成并注入冻结的LLM中；支持激活空间分类，通过选择重建能量最低的文本标签实现分类。

Result: 在三个目标LLM上的实验表明，UniSteer为行为控制、真实性引导、细粒度概念引导、多约束指令遵循和激活空间分类提供了统一的接口，展现了强大的跨任务控制能力。

Conclusion: UniSteer提出了一种灵活、统一的LLM控制框架，无需任务特定模块即可处理多种控制场景，为激活空间干预提供了更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Activation-based control steers large language models (LLMs) by intervening on their internal representations during inference, and has emerged as an effective paradigm for controlling behaviors such as persona and style. However, existing methods often rely on fixed steering directions or task-specific intervention modules, making them difficult to adapt to fine-grained concepts and compositional constraints. We propose UniSteer, a text-guided activation flow matching model that learns a conditional distribution over residual-stream activations from natural-language conditions. Instead of fitting a separate intervention for each target behavior, UniSteer learns a universal conditional velocity field in activation space. At inference time, UniSteer performs flow inversion by partially transporting a source activation toward a latent state and regenerating it under a target textual condition before injecting it back into the frozen LLM. The same conditional model supports activation-space classification by selecting the textual label with the lowest reconstruction energy. Experiments on three target LLMs show that UniSteer provides a unified interface across behavioral control, truthfulness steering, fine-grained concept steering, multi-constraint instruction following, and activation-space classification.

</details>


### [59] [CCS: Clinical Consensus Selection for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30131)
*Xi Zhang,Yingshu Li,Zaiqiao Meng,Jake Lever,Edmond S. L. Ho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出临床共识选择框架CCS，通过多候选采样和基于临床共识及图像嵌入的评估机制，提升医学影像报告生成的推理时质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像报告生成（RRG）通常被建模为单路径生成任务，现有进展主要依赖数据规模、模型容量和检索机制，但推理时报告质量的提升仍被忽视。作者观察到固定医学多模态大语言模型（MLLM）往往在其他候选报告中生成比默认解码结果更高质量的报告，表明推理时决策仍是未被充分关注的瓶颈。

Method: Clinical Consensus Selection（CCS）是一种解码器无关的推理时选择框架：首先采样多个候选报告，然后基于两个评估维度进行选择——文本层面的临床共识以及图像-报告训练的多模态嵌入器计算得到的图像导向效用（超越表面文本相似性的候选报告一致性度量）。

Result: 在三个数据集和多个医学MLLM上验证，CCS在推理时性能上持续优于单路径解码和通用Best-of-N基线，尤其在临床指标上有显著提升；分析表明图像导向效用与文本共识是相互独立的评估轴，且推理时仍有很大提升空间。

Conclusion: 推理时决策是医学影像报告生成中的一个关键瓶颈；CCS通过在推理时引入基于临床共识和图像嵌入的评估机制，有效缓解了该瓶颈，并为进一步优化推理时性能提供了明确的改进方向。

Abstract: Radiology report generation (RRG) is commonly formulated as a single-path generation task, where a multimodal large language model (MLLM) produces one decoded report as the final output. While recent progress has largely been driven by scaling training data, model capacity, and retrieval mechanisms, improving report quality at inference time remains underexplored. In this work, we observe that fixed radiology MLLMs often generate clinically stronger reports elsewhere in their candidate pool than the one selected by default decoding, suggesting that inference-time decision making remains an overlooked bottleneck. To address this, we propose Clinical Consensus Selection (CCS), a decoder-agnostic inference-time selection framework that samples multiple candidate reports and selects the one with the highest clinical consensus across the rollout pool. CCS unifies text-based utilities with a radiology-adapted utility computed by an image--report-trained multimodal embedder, which measures candidate agreement beyond surface-level textual similarity. Across three datasets and multiple radiology MLLMs, CCS consistently improves inference-time performance over single-path decoding and generic Best-of-N baselines, with particularly clear gains on clinical metrics. Further analysis shows that image-grounded utility forms a selection axis distinct from textual consensus and that substantial headroom remains for improving RRG at inference time.

</details>


### [60] [GRUFF: LLM Pronoun Fidelity, Reasoning, and Biases in German](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30214)
*Fabian Mewes,Anne Lauscher,Vagrant Gautam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GRUFF德语数据集，测量德语语言模型中代词保真度，发现LLM在德语阳性/阴性实体上有强语法一致性但对新代词表现不佳，编码器模型在德语中比英语更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有pronoun fidelity研究集中于英语，而德语具有更复杂的性别语法系统（四种名词性别、多种代词），需要研究语言模型在非英语环境下的指代推理和性别偏见。

Method: 构建GRUFF大规模德语数据集，包含四种名词性别系统和四组代词，测试LLM在无明确语境下的语法一致性、对干扰项的鲁棒性以及职业刻板印象的跨格相关性。

Result: LLM在无语境时对阳性/阴性实体有强语法一致性但对新代词xier和en表现不佳；模型对干扰项不够鲁棒；编码器模型在德语中比英语更鲁棒；职业刻板印象与语法格和大多数模型的相关性较低。

Conclusion: 德语语言模型在指代推理和性别偏见方面表现出独特的模式，编码器模型在德语中更鲁棒；数据集和代码开源以促进德语性别包容性语言和指代推理研究。

Abstract: Third-person singular pronouns have long been used to study stereotypical biases in language models and to test their abilities to reason about reference. More recently, the interplay between reasoning and bias has been investigated with the task of pronoun fidelity, which assesses models' abilities to correctly reuse a previously-specified pronoun for a discourse entity, independent of other potentially distracting discourse entities mentioned in between. However, such research focuses on English, which is a language with limited grammatical gender and almost no gender agreement. In this paper we contribute a novel, large-scale dataset, GRUFF, to measure pronoun fidelity in German, covering four different gender agreement systems in nouns, and four sets of pronouns. With this dataset, we show that LLMs show strong grammatical agreement for masculine and feminine entities in the absence of explicit context, but not for neopronouns xier and en. Models are generally not robust to distractors, but encoder-only models are more robust in German than in English, reflecting the importance of grammatical gender. Finally, we show that occupational stereotypes in this context are poorly correlated across grammatical cases, and across most models, except ones with closely related architectures. We release all code and data to encourage further work on gender-inclusive language and referential reasoning in German.

</details>


### [61] [Knowing What to Solve Before How: Preplan Empowered LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30245)
*Shaojie Wang,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PPC框架，在LLM推理中引入显式的"预规划"阶段，形成问题→预规划→规划→思维链的新范式，在数学推理任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于规划的推理方法采用"问题→规划→思维链"范式，但存在范式级缺陷：规划阶段和执行阶段都关注"如何解决"，而"解决什么"（问题类型、适用工具、潜在陷阱）完全隐式，导致推理质量受限。

Method: 提出PPC框架，引入显式的预规划阶段；采用三阶段合成流水线，通过spoiler-score检测器过滤泄露和spoiler失败以构建干净的预规划监督，使用复合GRPO奖励确保生成的规划真正遵循预规划。

Result: 在4个基座模型和5个数学推理基准测试中，PPC在40个指标中有39个取得最佳结果，相比最强基线提升maj@16 2.23%和pass@16 3.06%，且不增加推理token开销。

Conclusion: PPC通过在"解决什么"之前先明确"解决什么"，有效弥合了推理范式的结构性缺陷，为提升LLM推理能力提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Current plan-based reasoning methods improve large language models (LLMs) by inserting a planning stage before execution, giving rise to the question $\rightarrow$ plan $\rightarrow$ cot paradigm. While effective, a closer examination reveals an inherent paradigm-level gap: both the planning and its execution stages decide how to solve a problem, while the prior question of what to solve; recognizing the problem type, the applicable tools, and the foreseeable pitfalls; remains entirely implicit. To bridge this gap, we propose PPC (Preplan-Plan-CoT), a framework that introduces an explicit problem-understanding stage, the preplan, yielding a new question $\rightarrow$ preplan $\rightarrow$ plan $\rightarrow$ cot paradigm. Realizing this paradigm requires safeguarding the conceptual integrity of preplan at both ends. Specifically, we design a three-stage synthesis pipeline with a spoiler-score detector that filters out leakage and spoiler failures to build clean preplan supervision, and a composite GRPO reward enforces that the generated plan genuinely follows from the preplan. Experiments across four backbones and five mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that PPC achieves the best results on 39 of 40 metrics, improving maj@16 and pass@16 by +2.23 and +3.06 over the strongest baseline without introducing additional inference token overhead.

</details>


### [62] [Same Evidence, Different Answers: Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30251)
*Zizhuo Lin,Quanling Liu,Jinsheng Quan,Chao Zhang,Yifan Zhu,Xing Shi,Jingtao Xu,Zhihui Li,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为CCOPD（Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation）的训练方法，通过让模型在多轮对话中逐步接收信息的同时与全语境教师模型对齐，解决了LLM在渐进式信息揭示场景下的性能下降问题。实验表明，CCOPD在数学问题和五个零样本跨域任务上实现了32%的平均相对性能提升，同时保持了全语境性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在获得完整提示时能成功完成任务，但在相同信息分多轮逐步揭示时表现较差。作者认为这主要是由于"自锚定漂移"现象：模型在部分信息条件下生成的响应引入了未经支持的假设，这些假设会扭曲最终的答案，导致模型在全语境和分步语境下表现不一致。

Method: Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation（CCOPD）训练方法的核心思想是让同一基础模型在训练中扮演两种角色：作为冻结的教师模型接收完整清洁提示（FULL prompt），以及作为可训练的学生模型通过多轮对话逐步接收相同证据。CCOPD通过蒸馏机制使学生在自己的轨迹上的行为与教师在全语境下的行为对齐。

Result: 在数学问题对话数据集上训练后，CCOPD在RAW-SHARDED场景下的性能相比原始基础模型提升了32%。这种提升在数学问题和五个零样本跨域任务类别上都有体现，同时保持了全语境性能。进一步分析表明，CCOPD增强了模型对用户证据的锚定能力，减少了对早期助手回复的污染敏感性。

Conclusion: CCOPD成功缓解了LLM在渐进式信息揭示场景下的性能差距，通过在训练过程中强制模型保持与全语境教师行为的一致性，有效抑制了"自锚定漂移"现象。该方法不仅提高了分步场景下的性能，还保持了全语境性能，为提升LLM在多轮对话中的可靠性和一致性提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often solve a task when all instructions are given in a single prompt, but fail when the same information is revealed gradually across turns. When a clean FULL prompt and a RAW-SHARDED conversation contain the same complete user evidence, the model should still arrive at the same answer. We argue that a key reason for this gap is self-anchored drift: responses produced under partial information introduce unsupported assumptions, and those assumptions later distort the final answer. To reduce this effect, we propose Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation (CCOPD). During training, the same base model is used in two roles: a frozen teacher conditioned on the clean FULL prompt and a trainable student that receives the same evidence incrementally through a multi-turn conversation; CCOPD aligns the student's behavior on its own trajectories with the teacher's canonical full-context behavior. Trained only on math problem conversations, CCOPD yields a 32\% average relative improvement in RAW-SHARDED performance over the original base model across math and five zero-shot out-of-domain task families, while largely preserving full-context performance. Further analyses suggest that CCOPD strengthens grounding in user evidence and reduces sensitivity to contamination from earlier assistant turns.

</details>


### [63] [How LoRA Remembers? A Parametric Memory Law for LLM Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30260)
*Ziwen Xu,Haiwen Hong,Linsong Yu,Benglei Cui,Longtao Huang,Hui Xue,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过将LoRA作为潜在空间中的可控记忆容量探针，提出“参数记忆定律”，量化了LLM的确切参数记忆能力，并揭示了预测概率p＞0.5时即可实现逐字回忆的确定性相变。基于这些发现，作者提出了MemFT阈值引导优化策略，在实证评估中显著提升了记忆保真度和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM需要在动态真实环境中持续学习更新知识。虽然LoRA被广泛用于记忆更新，但现有研究主要依赖定性的下游评估，对参数记忆的确切容量限制和潜在动态机制缺乏定量探索。

Method: 1. 将LoRA作为潜在空间中的可控记忆容量探针，系统量化确切参数记忆；2. 提出“参数记忆定律”，建立损失减少ΔL与有效参数数量及序列长度的幂律关系；3. 在token级别进行细粒度分析，揭示确定性相变；4. 引入MemFT阈值引导优化策略，动态重新分配训练预算。

Result: 1. 证实预测概率p＞0.5是贪婪解码下逐字回忆的充分条件；2. 揭示了损失减少与参数/序列长度之间的幂律关系；3. MemFT在提升记忆保真度和效率方面表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了LLM参数记忆的定量理论框架，为理解记忆容量限制和优化记忆更新提供了新视角。MemFT策略证明了基于阈值引导的训练预算分配可以有效提升记忆性能，为LLM持续学习提供了可操作的方法论。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) must continuously learn and update knowledge to remain effective in dynamic real-world environments. While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is widely used for such memory updates, existing studies mainly rely on qualitative downstream evaluations, leaving the quantitative capacity limits and underlying dynamics of exact parametric memory largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we employ LoRA as a controlled memory capacity probe within the latent space to systematically quantify exact parametric memory. We introduce the Parametric Memory Law, a robust power law linking loss reduction Delta L to effective parameters and sequence length. At the token level, fine-grained analysis reveals a deterministic phase transition, demonstrating that a prediction probability of p > 0.5 constitutes a sufficient condition for verbatim recall under greedy decoding. Driven by these insights, we introduce MemFT, a threshold-guided optimization strategy that dynamically redistributes the training budget toward sub-threshold tokens. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that MemFT can enhance memory fidelity and efficiency. Code will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/ParametricMemoryLaw.

</details>


### [64] [Loong: A Human-Like Long Document Translation Agent with Observe-and-Act Adaptive Context Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30274)
*Yutong Wang,Xuebo Liu,Derek F. Wong,Zhilin Li,Rongqing Jiang,Min Zhang,Shimin Tao,Daimeng Wei,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Loong的类人长文档翻译智能体，利用3E记忆模块（本质-范例-实体）和强化学习来优化上下文选择策略，在多种语言方向上显著提升了翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档级翻译是大型语言模型面临的最大挑战之一，受限于上下文窗口的长度，无法有效处理全局连贯性，同时冗余的上下文信息还会降低翻译质量。

Method: 提出Loong智能体，采用3E记忆模块（本质-范例-实体）存储历史上下文，通过强化学习优化上下文选择策略，利用自身采样的观察-行动轨迹生成偏好数据进行策略优化。

Result: 在英汉、德语、法语等方向上实现了显著的质量提升，平均增益达13.0分，表现出强大的领域泛化能力、抗噪鲁棒性以及在超长文档翻译中的稳定性。

Conclusion: Loong通过自适应上下文选择和强化学习优化，有效解决了文档级翻译的上下文窗口限制和冗余信息问题，为长文档翻译提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Document-level translation remains one of the most challenging tasks for large language models, which are constrained by limited context windows that impede global cohesion, while simultaneously suffering from redundant contextual information that degrades translation quality. To address this, we propose a human-like long document translation agent called Loong, which leverages a 3E memory module (Essence-Exemplar-Entity) to store summaries, sentence pairs, and entity records as historical context. Instead of passively attending to all history, Loong performs deep reasoning to adaptively identify the optimal context for translation guidance. Loong optimizes its context policy through reinforcement learning, utilizing preference data derived from its own sampled observe-and-act reasoning trajectories. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that Loong achieves substantial translation quality improvements in English $\Leftrightarrow$ Chinese, German, and French directions, with average gains of up to 13.0 points across the three evaluation metrics. Furthermore, Loong exhibits strong generalization across domains and robustness against contextual noise, while maintaining remarkable stability in ultra-long document translation. Our code is released at https://github.com/YutongWang1216/LoongDocMT.

</details>


### [65] [MedCase-Structured: A Text-to-FHIR Dataset for Benchmarking Diagnostic Reasoning in Clinically Realistic EHR Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30295)
*Valentina Bui Muti,Eugénie Dulout,Ziquan Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种从非结构化临床文本生成HL7 FHIR R4结构化数据的流水线，用于LLM临床决策支持系统的现实评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在临床推理方面展现出潜力，但现有基准测试主要依赖静态数据集或非结构化输入，未能反映临床系统中使用的结构化、可互操作的数据格式，导致评估结果与实际部署环境存在偏差

Method: 采用分阶段LLM生成结合术语验证和修复的方法，减少幻觉代码并确保结构和语义一致性；将该流程应用于MedCaseReasoning构建MedCase-Structured数据集

Result: 在MedCase-Structured数据集上，82.5%的病例成功生成有效FHIR结构；评估显示，LLM在结构化FHIR输入上的诊断准确率显著低于纯文本输入

Conclusion: 强调了部署对齐基准测试的重要性，表明临床决策支持系统评估需要采用与实际部署环境一致的数据格式

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show promise for clinical reasoning and decision support, but evaluation in realistic, electronic health record-congruent settings remains limited. Existing benchmarks often rely on static datasets or unstructured inputs that do not reflect the structured, interoperable data formats used in clinical systems. We introduce a pipeline for generating clinically realistic HL7 FHIR R4 bundles from unstructured text, enabling controllable evaluation of clinical decision support systems. The pipeline combines staged LLM generation with terminology-grounded validation and repair to reduce hallucinated codes and enforce structural and semantic consistency. Applying this approach to MedCaseReasoning, we construct MedCase-Structured, a synthetic dataset aligned with clinician-authored diagnostic cases, achieving valid FHIR generation for 82.5% of cases. Evaluation on MedCase-Structured reveals consistently lower diagnostic accuracy for LLMs on structured FHIR inputs than with plain text, highlighting the importance of deployment-aligned benchmarking.

</details>


### [66] [Unlocking the Working Memory of Large Language Models for Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30343)
*Lukas Aichberger,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RiM是一种新型推理方法，通过固定记忆块替代自回归生成，使大语言模型能够利用工作记忆进行高效的潜在推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过生成中间token来扩展测试时计算量，但这种方法将推理与自回归生成耦合，混淆了内部计算与外部通信。人类认知可以使用工作记忆在内部保持和处理信息而不需要外化中间想法，因此需要一种新的推理范式。

Method: 引入RiM方法，用固定序列的特殊token（记忆块）替代自回归生成的推理步骤，这些记忆块解锁大语言模型的工作记忆容量。采用两阶段课程：1) 通过在每个记忆块后预测显式推理步骤来接地；2) 丢弃步骤级监督，在每个记忆块后迭代优化最终答案。

Result: 在推理基准测试中，RiM在不同家族和规模的语言模型上匹配或超过现有的潜在推理方法，且避免了思想的自回归生成。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可以被训练为将工作记忆作为一种有效的潜在推理机制。

Abstract: To improve the reasoning capabilities of large language models, test-time compute is typically scaled by generating intermediate tokens before the final answer. However, this couples reasoning to autoregressive generation and thereby conflates internal computation with external communication. In contrast, human cognition can use working memory to hold and manipulate information internally without the need to externalize intermediate thoughts. Drawing on this principle, we introduce Reasoning in Memory (RiM), a latent reasoning method that replaces the autoregressive generation of reasoning steps with memory blocks. These memory blocks are fixed sequences of special tokens that unlock the working-memory capacity of large language models. Since they are fixed rather than generated, they can be processed in a single forward pass, enabling compute-efficient latent reasoning. To operationalize these memory blocks, we employ a two-stage curriculum. First, we ground them by predicting explicit reasoning steps after each memory block. Second, we discard this step-level supervision and iteratively refine the final answer after each memory block. Our experiments on reasoning benchmarks show that, across language models of different families and sizes, RiM matches or exceeds existing latent reasoning methods while avoiding the autoregressive generation of thoughts. These results demonstrate that large language models can be trained to use working memory as an effective mechanism for latent reasoning.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [67] [Phase-Conditioned Imitation Learning with Autonomous Failure Recovery for Robust Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29407)
*Dayuan Chen,Kai Tang,Yukuan Zhang,Kazuhiro Kosuge,Yasuhisa Hirata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于相位条件与力感知的鲁棒可变形物体操作框架，通过FiLM条件编码器实现阶段自适应策略，结合多模态相位预测与混合阻抗控制器，将挂衣成功率从56%提升至87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模仿学习策略（如ACT）在推理时假设马尔可夫性，导致状态混淆问题：当视觉相似的观测需要矛盾动作时，无法区分并自主恢复执行失败。

Method: 1) FiLM条件ACT编码器，根据任务阶段调制特征提取，实现跨阶段共享动作动力学；2) 多模态相位预测器融合视觉、力、姿态反馈，实时检测接触故障并触发恢复轨迹；3) 混合阻抗控制器实现柔顺执行；4) 力觉遥操作界面用于数据收集。

Result: 消融实验表明FiLM调制显著优于无条件及token级条件基线，t-SNE分析证实FiLM诱导出良好分离的相位特定特征表示。在双臂挂衣场景中，闭环系统通过自主错误恢复将挂衣成功率从56%提升至87%。

Conclusion: 该工作通过相位条件与力感知的结合，有效解决了可变形物体操作中的状态混淆与执行失败恢复问题，为复杂操作任务提供了可泛化的鲁棒框架。

Abstract: This paper presents a phase-conditioned, force-aware framework for robust deformable object manipulation. Standard imitation learning policies such as Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) rely on a Markovian assumption at inference, causing state aliasing when visually similar observations require contradictory actions and preventing autonomous recovery from execution failures. We address this with a closed-loop hierarchical architecture. A FiLM-conditioned ACT encoder modulates feature extraction based on the current task phase, enabling a single unified policy to produce phase-specific behaviors while sharing action dynamics across phases. A multi-modal phase predictor fusing visual, force, and pose feedback estimates the phase in real time, detecting contact failures that are invisible to vision alone and autonomously triggering recovery trajectories. The system is completed by a hybrid impedance controller for compliant execution and a haptic teleoperation interface for force-aware data collection. Ablation studies show that FiLM-based modulation significantly outperforms both unconditioned and token-level conditioned baselines, and t-SNE analysis confirms that FiLM induces well-separated, phase-specific feature representations. Validated on hanging and removing a T-shirt with dual arms, the closed-loop system improves the hanging success rate from 56\% to 87\% through autonomous error recovery. Code and videos: https://leledeyuan00.github.io/phaser/

</details>


### [68] [ElegantVLA: Learning When to Think for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29438)
*Ye Li,Huanan Liu,Kangye Ji,Yuan Meng,Jiajun Fan,Yuansong Wang,Shiyu Qin,Chenglei Wu,Shu-Tao Xia,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ElegantVLA 是一种插拔式阶段自适应推理框架，通过内部模型动态计算调度加速 VLA 模型，无需修改或重训练基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型计算成本高、控制频率低，现有加速方法通常优化单个组件或依赖固定加速规则，将不同控制步骤视为均匀计算并忽视了序列具身控制中非均匀的推理需求。

Method: 提出轻量级调度器，通过观察时序表示相似性、机器人运动线索和任务进展，在视觉编码器、LLM 和动作头之间联合分配计算。感知-语言推理采用五级 Vision-LLM 计算模式，动作生成采用三级去噪模式，在稳定运动时重用中间去噪状态，在目标敏感阶段保持完全细化。

Result: 在 GR00T 和 CogACT 上分别实现 2.55x 和 3.77x 加速；在六个真实世界 GR00T 任务上减少计算 2.18x，并将控制频率从 13.8 Hz 提升至 26.3 Hz。

Conclusion: ElegantVLA 提供了一种适用于现代 VLA 流水线的通用加速框架，能够根据任务阶段自适应分配计算资源，在不修改或重训练基础模型的情况下提升实时控制性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a powerful paradigm for generalist robotic control. However, their high computational cost and limited control frequency hinder real-time robotic manipulation, especially when large vision-language backbones and iterative action heads run at every control step. Existing VLA acceleration methods often optimize individual components or rely on fixed acceleration rules, treating different control steps with largely fixed computation and overlooking the non-uniform reasoning demands of sequential embodied control. Inspired by human motor control, where cognitive and feedback resources concentrate on goal-sensitive stages, we argue that VLA models should learn when to invest full computation and when to reuse prior computation. We propose ElegantVLA, a plug-in phase-adaptive inference framework that accelerates VLA models through intra-model dynamic compute scheduling. ElegantVLA introduces a lightweight scheduler that observes temporal representation similarity, robot-motion cues, and episode progress to jointly allocate computation across the vision encoder, LLM, and action head. For perception-language reasoning, the scheduler selects a five-level Vision-LLM compute mode, from full recomputation to multi-step temporal reuse, based on visual-language representation stability. For action generation, it selects a three-level denoising mode, reusing intermediate denoising states during stable motion while preserving full refinement for goal-sensitive stages. By coordinating these decisions, ElegantVLA offers a general acceleration framework for modern VLA pipelines with explicit action-generation modules, without modifying or retraining the base model. Experiments on GR00T and CogACT achieve up to 2.55x and 3.77x speedup, and on six real-world GR00T tasks ElegantVLA cuts computation by 2.18x while raising control frequency from 13.8 Hz to 26.3 Hz.

</details>


### [69] [LLM-Guided Future Hypotheses for Horizon-Aware Exploration in Multi-Step Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29864)
*Mohammad Khoshnazar,Andrew Melnik,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Future-Experience Conditioning (FEC)框架，利用短期未来视频作为结构化先验来改善机器人操作的不确定性处理和策略适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步机器人操作需要在场景演变的不确定性下执行任务，这使得探索和策略适应具有挑战性。

Method: Future-Experience Conditioning (FEC) 接口，将闭环策略条件化于短期未来视频的潜在表示。未来视频生成包含三个阶段：(1) LLM推理器基于任务本体运行，(2) 机器人无关数字孪生对预期物体运动的模拟，(3) 无遮罩视频扩散模型合成与机器人一致的未来视频。主要用BC和BC+RL实现。

Result: 生成的未来视频比无未来条件化的性能更好，而不匹配的未来视频会降低性能。BC+RL实现取得了最强整体结果。在8个CALVIN任务上，GTFuture改进最快，GenFuture比NoFuture更早且达到更高水平，WrongFuture在整个训练中保持为零。

Conclusion: 短期未来视频可以作为探索和政策适应的有用结构化先验，即使未来预测不完美。

Abstract: Multi-step robot manipulation requires acting under uncertainty about how the scene will evolve, making exploration and policy adaptation challenging. We study whether short-horizon, task-consistent future videos can provide useful structured priors for control and reinforcement-learning fine-tuning. We formalize this idea through Future-Experience Conditioning (FEC), a simple interface that conditions closed-loop policies on a latent representation of a short future video. In our simulation setup, future clips are generated in three stages, an LLM reasoner operating over a task ontology initialized from the current scene state, a robot-free digital-twin rollout of the intended object motion, and a mask-free video diffusion model that synthesizes a robot-consistent future clip without requiring segmentation at inference. We instantiate this future-conditioning interface primarily with BC and BC+RL, and compare against a future-conditioned Streaming Flow Policy (SFP) baseline on RoboCasa and CALVIN under NoFuture, GTFuture, GenFuture, and WrongFuture. Generated futures improve performance over no-future conditioning, while mismatched futures degrade it, and our BC+RL instantiation achieves the strongest overall results. An average BC+RL learning-curve analysis across 8 CALVIN tasks further shows that GTFuture improves fastest, GenFuture improves earlier and to a higher level than NoFuture, and WrongFuture remains at zero throughout training. These results suggest that short-horizon future videos can serve as useful structured priors for exploration and policy adaptation under imperfect future predictions. https://enact2026.github.io/

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [70] [VFEAgent: A Multimodal Agent Framework for End-to-End Automated Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28978)
*Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Chenghao Liu,Siyuan Liu,Shixin Wu,Linsen Zhang,Boyu Wang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VFEAgent是一个端到端多智能体系统，能够从图像和问题描述自动完成有限元分析建模和仿真，采用多模态视觉-语言推理和验证优先的代码合成框架，显著优于现有LLM基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析（FEA）工作流复杂且高度依赖领域专业知识。现有将大语言模型（LLM）集成到FEA中的方法在处理多模态输入和执行复杂任务方面存在局限性。

Method: 核心包含两个组件：(1) 多模态视觉-语言多智能体流水线，采用ReAct驱动的推理从异构输入中提取结构化FEA规范；(2) 验证优先的代码合成框架，集成健壮的自调试和回退机制以确保可执行性和物理有效性。

Result: 在多种工程力学场景下系统性评估显示，VFEAgent在生成完整且物理有效的仿真方面成功率很高，在可靠性和正确性方面优于基于LLM的基线方法。

Conclusion: 验证了自动化完整FEA工作流的可行性，突出了该框架解放工程师免于繁琐手动分析的潜力。

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) serves as the cornerstone of modern engineering design. However, its workflow is inherently complex and relies heavily on domain expertise. Although recent efforts have integrated Large Language Models (LLMs) into FEA, existing approaches face limitations in handling multimodal inputs and executing complex tasks. To address these limitations, we propose VFEAgent, an end-to-end multi-agent system designed to automate FEA modeling and simulation directly from input images and problem descriptions. Our methodology integrates two core components: (1) a multimodal vision-language multi-agent pipeline that employs ReAct-driven reasoning to extract structured FEA specifications from heterogeneous inputs and (2) a verification-first code synthesis framework, incorporating robust self-debugging and fallback mechanisms to ensure executability and physical validity. We systematically evaluated the system across various engineering mechanics scenarios. The results demonstrate that VFEAgent achieves a high success rate in generating complete and physically valid simulations, outperforming LLM-based baseline methods in reliability and correctness. These findings validate the feasibility of automating the complete FEA workflow, highlighting the framework's potential to liberate engineers from tedious manual analysis.

</details>


### [71] [BEAMS: Benchmarking and Evaluating AI for Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28994)
*Sara Metcalf,William Schoenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BEAMS倡议通过建立基准测试和开源平台，评估AI建模工具的伦理性与可解释性，发现其在定性任务和讨论方面表现较好，但在因果推理和定量错误修复方面存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工具用于现实世界决策时，必须能够构建具有解释性的仿真模型，且应补充而非替代人类专业知识。BEAMS倡议旨在引导AI建模工具向负责任、合乎伦理的方向发展，建立以人为中心建模实践的基准。

Method: BEAMS倡议使用开源的"sd ai"项目，建立开放数字和组织基础设施，通过协作方式评估AI建模工具。设立指导小组确定优先级，技术小组将基准测试实施为自动化测试，涵盖因果翻译、模型迭代、因果推理、一致性、模型行为解释、建议建模步骤和模型修复建议等类别。

Result: 不同LLM在相同引擎类型下表现存在差异。AI建模工具在讨论和基本定性任务上表现较好，而在因果推理和定量错误修复方面表现较差。没有单一LLM在所有任务上占主导地位，凸显了特定任务的重要性以及速度与准确性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 倡议正致力于纳入解决偏见问题的基准测试，通过考虑不同视角和以人为中心的使用案例来提升AI工具的公平性与实用性，确保工具真正服务于人类专家的决策过程。

Abstract: AI tools to support real world decision making must be able to build simulation models that inform their recommendations and render them interpretable. Tools that can automate aspects of modeling practice must complement human expertise, not replace it. The BEAMS Initiative aims to guide the development of AI tools for modeling and simulation toward forms that are responsible and ethical by establishing benchmarks for human centered modeling and simulation practices. The initiative uses open digital and organizational infrastructure to collaboratively evaluate AI tools for modeling and simulation. The open source sd ai project hosted by the initiative establishes transparency and enables contributions to be shared broadly. A steering group focuses on prioritizing potential benchmarks, while a technical group focuses on implementing the benchmarks in the form of automated tests. Tests for several distinct categories of evaluation have been implemented and applied to AI tools that support qualitative model building, quantitative model building, and model discussion. These include tests for causal translation, model iteration, causal reasoning, conformance, model behavior explanation, suggested model building steps, and suggested model fixes. When engines from the sd ai project are coupled with different LLMs, their performance on these evaluations reveals variability across different AI tools. The evaluations implemented by the initiative demonstrate that AI enabled modeling tools perform better at discussion and basic qualitative tasks than with causal reasoning and quantitative error fixing. No single LLM dominates across engine types, highlighting the importance of specific tasks and tradeoffs between speed and accuracy. Ongoing efforts of the initiative aim to incorporate benchmarks that address concerns about bias by considering alternative perspectives and human centered use cases.

</details>


### [72] [Mind Your Tone: Does Tone Alter LLM Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29027)
*Om Dobariya,Akhil Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了提示语语气变化对大语言模型在客观选择题上表现的影响，发现语气效应系统但高度依赖于模型，不同模型表现出不同程度的准确率波动，并提出了解释语气如何调节内部推理模式的路由框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLM)应用日益广泛，但其性能受提示语风格和语气影响。现有研究已观察到提示语语气对模型表现的影响，但缺乏对"是否存在"以及"如何影响"的系统性探究，特别是在客观选择题任务上的表现差异。

Method: 使用两个数据集：50题基础数据集(5种语气变体)和570题MMLU子集(57个学科，7种语气变体)。评估4个低成本流行LLM：ChatGPT-4o、ChatGPT-5-nano、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite，通过实验比较不同语气下的模型准确率。

Result: 语气效应系统但高度模型依赖：部分模型显示微小但统计学显著的变化，其他模型则呈现大幅准确率波动。识别出学科级别的语气敏感性差异，提出路由框架解释语气如何调节内部推理模式。

Conclusion: 研究发现警告用户不应假设LLM在部署中具有语气鲁棒性，提示语语气选择对模型性能有显著影响，需谨慎对待不同语气场景下的模型可靠性。

Abstract: The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) is proliferating, yet their performance is observed to vary based on prompting styles and tones. In this study, we investigate both whether and how tonal variations in prompts lead to disparate LLM accuracy for objective multiple-choice questions. We use two datasets: a 50-base question dataset with five tone variants and a 570-base question MMLU subset spanning 57 subjects with seven tone variants. Experiments were conducted to evaluate the performance of four cost-efficient, popular LLMs: ChatGPT-4o, ChatGPT-5-nano, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. Across models, tonal effects are systematic but highly model-dependent. Some models show small, yet statistically significant, shifts, while others exhibit large accuracy swings across tones. Further, we identify subject-level differences in tone sensitivity and present a routing framework to explain how tones may attune internal reasoning modes. Our findings caution users against assuming tone-robust reliability in LLM deployments.

</details>


### [73] [Practitioner Beliefs and Behaviors in AI-Enhanced Education: DOT Framework Survey Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29041)
*David Gibson,M. Elizabeth Azukas,Gerald Knezek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一项针对72名高等教育从业者的横断面调查，基于DOT框架探究AI在教学中的整合，识别出从业者信念的三个维度：AI功能能力、监督与治理、教师协作与规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高等教育从业者关于AI在教学与学习中整合的信念、行为及机构条件，为DOT框架提供初步实证支持，并识别理论与现实之间的差距。

Method: 采用横断面调查（n=72），基于DOT框架（整合设计思维和开放系统理论）考察AI熟悉度、使用模式、设计导向实践和教学信念；对19个信念项目进行探索性因子分析。

Result: 从业者对AI作为教学支持持积极态度，但强调人类监督和批判性评估；实践侧重迭代提示词和内容生成，需求评估和反馈循环使用不一致；普遍报告政策、培训和基础设施不足等机构障碍。

Conclusion: 为DOT框架作为从业者信念与实践的描述性模型提供了初步实证支持，揭示了设计导向理论与当前实施之间的差距；贡献了初步测量结构，并指出了确认性验证和基于结果的研究方向。

Abstract: This study reports findings from a cross-sectional survey (n = 72) of higher education practitioners examining beliefs, behaviors, and institutional conditions related to artificial intelligence (AI) integration in teaching and learning. Grounded in the DOT Framework, which integrates design thinking and open systems theory, the study investigates AI familiarity, usage patterns, design-oriented practices, and pedagogical beliefs. Exploratory factor analysis of 19 belief items identified a three-factor structure: AI Functional Capabilities, Oversight and Governance, and Instructor Collaboration and Planning (α = .90). Results indicate that practitioners hold favorable views of AI as a pedagogical support while maintaining strong commitments to human oversight and critical evaluation. Reported practices emphasize iterative prompting and content generation, with less consistent use of needs assessment and feedback loops. Institutional barriers including limited policy, training, and infrastructure were widely reported. These findings provide preliminary empirical support for the DOT Framework as a descriptive model of practitioner beliefs and practices, while also highlighting gaps between design-oriented theory and current implementation. The study contributes an initial measurement structure and identifies directions for confirmatory validation and outcome-based research linking AI-supported design practices to instructional quality.

</details>


### [74] [Hallucination Mitigation with Agentic AI, Nested Learning, and AI Sustainability via Semantic Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29055)
*Diego Gosmar,Deborah A. Dahl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于HOPE的嵌套学习架构与连续记忆系统相结合的多智能体LLM流水线，通过三层评审机制和语义缓存显著减少幻觉，在310个提示词的混合基准测试中实现-31.3%至-35.9%的端到端THS（总幻觉评分）降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉是生产级LLM系统的主要可靠性障碍，特别是在多智能体管道中，未支持的声明可能在不经检查的情况下在各阶段间传播，导致错误信息扩散。

Method: 采用HOPE启发的嵌套学习架构结合连续记忆系统（CMS）和语义相似性缓存，构建三层不对称智能体流水线：FrontEndAgent作为高随机性生成器（温度=1.0）产生幻觉基线，SecondLevelReviewer和ThirdLevelReviewer作为渐进式纠错器；基于Open Floor Protocol（OFP）编排，使用五项KPI（FCD、FGR、FDF、ECS、OSR）聚合为THS（总幻觉评分），其中FDF、ECS、OSR和FGR作为缓解信号。

Result: 在5种加权配置下，端到端THS降低31.3%-35.9%；语义缓存命中47.3%（440/930），LLM调用减少至490，降低能源和CO2e足迹；ExtremeObservability配置获得最负的THS（-0.0709），验证了高可见性配置可强化而非削弱缓解效果。

Conclusion: 记忆增强的多智能体设计可以在不重新训练模型的情况下同时提升事实可靠性、操作效率和可审计性，证实了可见性与缓解措施之间的协同效应。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a major reliability barrier for production LLM systems, particularly in multi-agent pipelines where unsupported claims can propagate unchecked across stages. This paper adapts a HOPE-inspired Nested Learning architecture with Continuum Memory Systems (CMS) and semantic similarity caching to a hybrid benchmark of 310 prompts combining 217 epistemic-uncertainty prompts and 93 fabrication-induction stress-test prompts. A three-stage agentic pipeline orchestrated via the Open Floor Protocol (OFP) is evaluated with five KPIs -- FCD (Factual Claim Density), FGR (Factual Grounding References), FDF (Fictional Disclaimer Frequency), ECS (Explicit Contextualization Score), and OSR (Observability Score Ratio) -- aggregated into THS (Total Hallucination Score) across five weighting configurations to study mitigation-observability trade-offs. FDF, ECS, OSR, and FGR are subtracted as mitigation signals, so that a more negative THS indicates stronger mitigation. The FrontEndAgent is configured as a high-stochasticity generator (temperature = 1.0) to produce a realistic hallucination baseline, while the SecondLevelReviewer and ThirdLevelReviewer operate as progressive correctors. This asymmetric design yields end-to-end THS reductions of -31.3% to -35.9% across five weighting configurations. Semantic caching achieves 440 cache hits over 930 potential calls (47.3% hit rate), reducing LLM invocations to 490, lowering energy and CO2e footprint, and making multi-stage review pipelines operationally viable at production scale. ExtremeObservability attains the most negative final THS (-0.0709), confirming that observability-heavy configurations reinforce rather than compromise mitigation. These findings suggest that memory-augmented multi-agent designs can jointly improve factual reliability, operational efficiency, and auditability without model retraining.

</details>


### [75] [Robust and Efficient Guardrails with Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29068)
*Siddharth Sai,Xiaofei Wen,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI
