TL;DR: 提出COLAGUARD，通过分阶段训练课程将多步安全推理转移到连续潜在空间，实现直接隐藏状态传播，在保持安全性的同时大幅提升推理效率和降低成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全护栏多采用单次分类或显式推理，显式推理虽性能更优但存在高查询延迟和令牌开销，难以满足高吞吐部署需求

Method: COLAGUARD采用分阶段训练课程，将多步安全推理转移到连续潜在空间，实现推理时直接隐藏状态传播，无需生成显式推理过程

Result: 在10个提示词和响应调节设置（8个安全基准）上，COLAGUARD比Llama Guard 3提升macro-F1 8.24个百分点，与GuardReasoner（显式推理基线）macro-F1持平，但实现12.9倍速度提升和22.4倍令牌使用减少

Conclusion: 潜在推理为可部署护栏提供了显式推理生成的高效替代方案，在提升安全鲁棒性的同时改善推理效率，而非将二者视为竞争目标

Abstract: Maintaining the safety of large language models (LLMs) is crucial as they are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. Existing safety guardrails typically rely on single-pass classification or, more recently, distilled reasoning. Reasoning-based guardrails significantly outperform classification-only baselines, but they incur substantial query latency and token overhead that make them impractical for highthroughput deployment. To address this challenge, we propose COLAGUARD, a guardrail model that transfers multi-step safety reasoning into a continuous latent space through a stage-wise training curriculum, enabling direct hidden-state propagation at inference. Evaluated on ten prompt- and response-moderation settings spanning eight safety benchmarks, COLAGUARD improves macro-F1 by 8.24 points over Llama Guard 3 and matches our explicit reasoning baseline, GuardReasoner, in macroF1 while delivering a 12.9X speedup and 22.4X reduction in token usage. Our results suggest that latent reasoning offers a practical alternative to explicit rationale generation for deployable guardrails, jointly improving safety robustness and inference efficiency rather than treating them as competing objectives.

</details>


### [76] [Trends in AI and Human-AI Interaction in Clinical Trials -- A Hybrid Human-AI Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29096)
*Sandra Woolley,Tim Collins,Khalid Khattak,Illia Chernomorets,Ariane Arevalo,Chris Richardson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析ClinicalTrials.gov注册库中的记录，探讨了AI相关临床试验的时间趋势和地理分布，并评估了混合人机工作流程在分析人机交互趋势中的可行性。研究显示AI相关临床试验数量显著增加，中国和美国位居前列，其他国家如意大利、法国、西班牙、英国和土耳其也呈现快速增长。混合人机筛查方法总体可行，但需要更清晰的试验报告和更精确的交互定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过临床试验注册库记录，表征AI术语的时间趋势和AI试验的地理分布，并探索一种探索性混合人机方法来分析注册临床试验中的人机交互趋势。

Method: 从ClinicalTrials.gov注册库检索记录，采用混合工作流程（前沿生成式AI模型GPT-5.5与人工审查相结合）来筛查和分类AI重点搜索返回的记录。

Result: 研究发现AI相关试验数量显著增加，机器学习、深度学习、聊天机器人、GPT和大语言模型的引用近期增长明显。地理上，中国和美国拥有最多的AI相关试验，意大利、法国、西班牙、英国和土耳其等国也呈现近期增长。在100条记录的随机样本中，人类和AI分类器在识别未实质性使用AI的研究方面显示出良好的一致性，但在分类人机交互方面一致性较低，特别是当卫生专业人员交互描述模糊或不充分时。

Conclusion: 混合人机筛查临床试验记录的方法总体具有可行性，但需要更清晰的试验报告标准和更精确的交互定义来提高过程效率。

Abstract: This paper examines records retrieved from the ClinicalTrials.gov registry to characterize temporal trends in AI terminology and the geographical distribution of AI trials. The work also reports on an exploratory hybrid human-AI approach to analyzing human-AI interaction trends in registered clinical trials. The hybrid workflow comprised a frontier generative AI model (GPT-5.5) and human review to screen and categorize records returned by an AI-focused search. The findings indicate a marked increase in AI-related trials over time, with recent growth in references to machine learning, deep learning, chatbots, GPTs, and large language models. Geographically, China and the United States accounted for the largest numbers of AI-related trials, with notable recent increases in several other countries including Italy, France, Spain, the UK and Turkey (Türkiye). In a random sample of 100 records, human and AI classifiers showed good agreement in identifying studies not substantively using AI, but lower agreement in classifying human-AI interaction, particularly where health professional interaction was ambiguous or insufficiently described. Overall, the results suggest that hybrid human-AI screening of clinical trial records is potentially viable, but clearer trial reporting and more precise interaction definitions will benefit the process.

</details>


### [77] [Better Later Than Sooner: Neuro-Symbolic Knowledge Graph Construction via Ontology-grounded Post-extraction Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29168)
*Lorenzo Loconte,Timothy Hospedales,Cristina Cornelio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号框架，通过开放域提取、嵌入式规范化和目标LLM校正来构建本体 grounded 知识图谱，在降低 token 使用量的同时提高图谱一致性并保持下游 QA 质量，证明提取的图谱适合符号查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂问答需要多跳推理和符号操作（如聚合、列举），图增强检索虽能部分解决但缺乏符号操作所需的结构；现有符号图谱方法因使用 LLM 提取事实，易违反常识本体约束导致一致性差，亟需一种兼顾图谱一致性、减少 LLM 调用成本的解决方案。

Method: 神经符号本体 grounded 图谱构建框架：结合开放域提取、基于嵌入的类型与谓词规范化以及有针对性的 LLM 校正；将校正推迟至提取后阶段，避免重复调用 LLM。

Result: 大幅减少 token 使用，提高图谱一致性并保持下游 QA 质量；提取的图谱适合符号查询（通过 SPARQL 图模式出现频率衡量）。

Conclusion: 所提神经符号框架能有效构建本体 grounded 知识图谱，在降低计算开销的同时提升一致性并维持问答性能，使其适用于符号查询任务。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) is a core challenge in AI, particularly for complex queries requiring multi-hop reasoning across documents, or symbolic operations like aggregation or exhaustive listing. Retrieval-augmented generation has become the dominant approach to QA, with recent graph-based variants addressing part of these issues by organizing knowledge to better support compositional questions. However, most textual graph-based RAG methods still lack the structure needed for symbolic operations useful to answer complex questions reliably. This motivates symbolic graph-based approaches, which extract knowledge graphs (KGs) whose relations are logic predicates that enable SQL-like querying. Yet these pipelines typically use LLMs for KG extraction, which can introduce consistency issues, where extracted facts may violate commonsense ontology constraints. We propose a neuro-symbolic framework for ontology-grounded KG construction combining open-domain extraction, embedding-based canonicalization of types and predicates, and targeted LLM-based correction of ontology violations. By deferring corrections to a post-extraction stage, our method avoids repeated LLM calls, substantially reducing token usage while improving KG consistency and preserving downstream QA quality. Finally, we show that the extracted KGs are well suited for symbolic querying by measuring the occurrence of SPARQL graph patterns.

</details>


### [78] [ReasonOps: Operator Segmentation for LLM Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29192)
*Daniel Lee,Owen Queen,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReasonOps是一种无监督、表达力强的方法，用于标注和解析大语言模型的思维链轨迹。通过分析44,662个思维链样本，发现7个通用的推理算子（如回溯、推断、假设等），这些算子跨模型和领域普遍存在，能够有效识别模型身份、预测答案正确性，并支持早期质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链轨迹往往长达数万token，但缺乏描述其内部结构的词汇。现有分析方法要么过于僵化，要么表达力不足，无法捕捉跨领域和跨模型的特征。

Method: ReasonOps通过无监督聚类句子开头的3-token枢轴词，发现7个重复出现的推理算子（如回溯、推断、假设等）。在12个思维LLM的44,662个轨迹上进行分析，覆盖6个模型家族和8个推理基准。

Result: 7个算子出现在所有模型家族和基准领域；独立LLM裁判对未见样本的分类准确率为70-76%；反射算子在难题上更有帮助，在简单题上反而损害性能；算子序列具有强模型识别能力；算子特征可预测答案正确性；在50%的轨迹长度时即可达到WP-AUC。

Conclusion: ReasonOps是一个无监督、无需标注的管道，能够深入洞察LLM推理轨迹的结构，同时在模型识别和正确性预测方面取得强大下游性能。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought traces from large reasoning models can span tens of thousands of tokens, yet we lack a vocabulary for describing their internal structure. Previous methods developed to analyze chain-of-thought traces are either too rigid or not expressive enough, failing to capture features across domains and models. To remedy this, we develop ReasonOps, an unsupervised, expressive method for annotating chain-of-thought traces, providing succinct universal operators. Using ReasonOps, we analyze 44,662 traces from 12 thinking LLMs spanning 6 families across 8 reasoning benchmarks and discover that they share a common compositional structure: 7 recurring reasoning operators -- discourse-level moves such as backtracking, inferring, and hypothesizing -- that emerge from unsupervised clustering of sentence-initial 3-token pivots. These operators appear across every model family and benchmark domain, confirmed by three independent LLM judges who classify held-out samples at 70 -76% accuracy. We analyze the structure of operators on easy vs. hard problems, revealing that reflective operators are more helpful on hard problems and harm performance on easy problems. Operator sequences are highly model-identifying: a classifier trained on operator distributions alone recovers the source model with macro-AUC, revealing that each model family has a distinctive reasoning fingerprint. Structural operator features predict within-problem answer correctness well above baselines. Classifiers built on these operators reach WP-AUC and on AIME specifically. ReasonOps further enables early quality estimation well before the trace completes: we predict at WP-AUC for only 50% of the trace. The ReasonOps pipeline is unsupervised and annotation-free, enabling deep insights into LLM reasoning traces as well as strong downstream results on model identification and correctness prediction.

</details>


### [79] [Tailoring the Curriculum: Student-Centered Reasoning Distillation via Dynamic Data-Model Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29229)
*Jiahao Huang,Fei Cheng,Junfeng Jiang,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于评估数据集与学生模型兼容性的新指标——数据-模型兼容性（DMC），用于优化大模型向小模型的推理蒸馏过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理蒸馏能够将大语言模型的复杂推理能力转移给小模型，但其成功依赖于训练数据与学生模型的匹配程度。现有方法缺乏有效的数据集评估标准，难以确定哪些数据最适合特定学生模型。

Method: 提出了数据-模型兼容性（DMC）指标，该指标通过联合考虑数据质量、相对难度和学生能力三个维度来评估数据集的适用性。此外，实验还验证了基于DMC进行动态数据选择的效果。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）DMC与推理蒸馏性能具有强相关性；2）使用DMC作为数据选择标准能显著提升蒸馏性能；3）基于DMC的动态数据选择能够进一步改善性能。这些发现在多个学生模型和任务上均得到一致验证。

Conclusion: DMC是一个有效的数据集评估指标，能够准确预测数据集与学生模型的兼容性。通过使用DMC进行静态和动态的数据选择，可以显著提升推理蒸馏的质量和效果。

Abstract: Reasoning distillation transfers complex reasoning abilities from large language models (LLMs) to smaller ones, yet its success depends on how well the training data align with the student model. This paper introduces the Data-Model Compatibility (DMC) metric, which can be used to assess the suitability of a dataset for reasoning distillation on a student model. DMC provides an assessment by jointly considering data quality, relative difficulty, and student capability. We validated the effectiveness of DMC from two perspectives: (1) DMC exhibits a strong correlation with reasoning distillation performance; and (2) using DMC as the criterion for data selection leads to improved reasoning distillation performance. Both findings are consistently demonstrated across multiple student models and tasks. Moreover, since the DMC of each dataset dynamically changes during training, our experiments demonstrate that dynamically selecting datasets based on DMC can further enhance performance.

</details>


### [80] [Surfacing Isolated Learners with Outcome-Independent Mediation of Feedback between Teachers and Students Using AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29240)
*Junsoo Park,Youssef Medhat,Htet Phyo Wai,Ploy Thajchayapong,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种可解释的决策层，通过整合学生学习困难普遍性、自我报告与观察到的困难之间的差异以及未解决的教师关切三个信号，在无成绩或事后标签的情况下对需要关注的教学主题进行排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI增强课堂在成绩发布前会产生丰富的教师和学生反馈信号，但这些信号难以转化为及时的教学决策。

Method: 该方法结合三个信号：1) 学生学习困难普遍性；2) 学习者自我报告与观察到的困难之间的差异；3) 未解决的教师关切。输出为排序的主题优先级列表，每个主题附带决策记录解释排序原因。

Result: 在一门研究生CS课程（n=5名教师访谈；n=279份调查问卷）中：优先排序的主题与教师关切高度一致（前5项重叠3/5；Spearman ρ=0.80）；与学生报告的主题难度相关（ρ=0.46，p=.048）。多信号整合比单一信号（仅困难普遍性）能发现更多学习者（AUC=0.96 vs 0.91）。反思思维、求助行为和自我效能感提供了额外证据，表明学生行为信号与学习相关构念一致。

Conclusion: 这些发现表明，透明协调机制可能有助于在反馈不完整时支持人机协同，从而辅助教师做出及时的教学决策。

Abstract: AI-augmented classrooms generate rich teacher and student feedback before graded outcomes become available, yet these signals can be difficult to translate into timely instructional decisions. We propose an interpretable decision layer: a transparent mechanism that ranks course topics requiring attention without using grades or post-hoc outcome labels. The approach combines three signals: student learning difficulty prevalence, disagreement between learner self-reports and observed difficulties, and unresolved teacher concerns. The output is a ranked set of topic priorities with per-topic decision records explaining each ranking. In one graduate CS course offering ($n=5$ instructor interviews; $n=279$ survey responses), prioritized topics aligned with instructor concerns (top-5 overlap 3/5; Spearman $ρ=0.80$) and student-reported topic difficulty ($ρ=0.46$, $p=.048$). Multi-signal integration also surfaced learners not identified through individual signal sources alone (AUC $=0.96$ vs. $0.91$ for gap prevalence alone). Reflective thinking, help-seeking, and self-efficacy provided additional evidence that student behavioral signals align with learning-related constructs. While preliminary, these findings suggest that transparent coordination mechanisms may help support human-AI co-agency when feedback is incomplete.

</details>


### [81] [DenseSteer: Steering Small Language Models towards Dense Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29247)
*Yang Ouyang,Shuhang Lin,Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM具备强大的思维链推理能力，但小模型（≤3B参数）在多步推理任务中表现不佳。DenseSteer是一种训练无关的推理时引导框架，通过调节内部表示以促进“密集推理”模式，从而提升小模型推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于对Qwen-2.5模型家族在数学推理基准上的实证分析，研究发现推理能力更强的模型具有更少的推理步骤但更高的每步信息密度，这种特性被称为“密集推理”。

Method: 提出DenseSteer训练无关推理时引导框架，通过调节内部表示使小模型采用密集推理模式，无需训练即可在推理时提升性能。

Result: 实验表明该方法在保持token级负对数似然不变的情况下，实现了持续的准确率提升，证明了密集推理作为数学问题求解有效结构方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 密集推理是数学问题求解的有效结构方法，DenseSteer提供了一种无需训练的推理时引导机制来增强小模型推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning abilities, while smaller models (<= 3B parameters) significantly underperform on multi-step reasoning tasks. Based on empirical analyses of the Qwen-2.5 model family on math reasoning benchmarks, we find that more proficient reasoning is associated with fewer reasoning steps but higher information density per step, a property we term Dense Reasoning. Motivated by this observation, we propose DenseSteer, a training-free inference-time steering framework that enhances small-model reasoning by modulating internal representations toward dense reasoning patterns. Experiments show that our method yields consistent accuracy improvements without increasing token-level Negative Log-Likelihood, highlighting dense reasoning as an effective structural approach to mathematical problem solving.

</details>


### [82] [Provably Secure Agent Guardrail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29251)
*Benlong Wu,Weiming Zhang,Kejiang Chen,Han Fang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型从生成引擎转变为具有广泛执行权限的智能体，AI失控引发AI安全危机。本文提出基于逻辑推理基本局限性的新安全范式，以及可执行的证明约束动作框架，通过神经符号隔离架构迫使智能体将意图无损形式化为第一阶逻辑数学约束。实验表明该形式验证机制在评估场景中实现零攻击成功率、零误报率，计算延迟极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型正从有界生成引擎转变为具有广泛执行权限的智能体，AI失控引发AI安全基本危机。现有防御架构依赖经验语义护栏和概率大模型裁决器，但这些机制在面对复杂语义符号解耦攻击时无法提供确定性安全下界。

Method: 提出基于逻辑推理基本局限性的新安全范式，引入可执行的证明约束动作框架，采用神经符号隔离架构。该框架放弃对自然语言的语义信任，迫使智能体将意图无损形式化为第一阶逻辑数学约束。

Result: 在宏观和微观二维动态对抗系统的实证评估中，形式验证机制实现零攻击成功率和零误报率，计算延迟极低。

Conclusion: 为未来智能系统构建底层防御基础提供条件形式化基础和工程范式，提供了在明确系统假设下的安全保证。

Abstract: As large language models transition from bounded generative engines to agents with expansive execution privileges, AI going out of control precipitates a fundamental crisis in artificial intelligence security. Existing defense architectures heavily rely on empirical semantic guardrails and probabilistic large model adjudicators, mechanisms that fail to provide deterministic security lower bounds when facing complex semantic symbol decoupling attacks. To overcome this empirical semantic guardrail dilemma, this paper proposes a new security paradigm for agents based on the fundamental limitations of logical reasoning. Based on this paradigm, we further introduce an executable Proof-Constrained Action (ePCA) framework with a neural symbolic isolation architecture. This framework abandons semantic trust in natural language, forcing agents to losslessly formalize their intentions into first-order logical mathematical constraints before performing physical operations. Empirical evaluations of macroscopic and microscopic two-dimensional dynamic adversarial systems demonstrate that our formal verification mechanism achieves zero attack success rate and zero false positive rate across the evaluated scenarios, with extremely low computational latency. This research provides a conditional formal foundation under explicit system assumptions and an engineering paradigm for constructing the underlying defense foundation for future intelligent systems.

</details>


### [83] [Entropy-KL Divergence-based Token Masking: A Novel Approach for Selective Fine-tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29303)
*Qi Liu,Mingdi Sun,Yongyi He,Zhi Zheng,Tong Xu,Yi Zheng,Zhefeng Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EKSFT方法，在低数据场景下通过选择性掩蔽高熵和高KL散度token，在保持预训练分布完整性的同时注入任务特定知识，从而改善RL探索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SFT+RL范式在小数据SFT初始化时，模型容易过拟合有限样本导致分布偏移，进而影响后续RL探索的效率。

Method: EKSFT（Entropy-KL Selective Fine-Tuning）通过选择性掩蔽策略，识别并排除既具有高熵又与参考模型存在高KL散度的token，避免模型模仿这些分布偏移较大的token，从而在注入任务知识的同时保持预训练分布的完整性。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，EKSFT在低数据场景下 consistently 优于标准SFT；基于EKSFT模型的后续RL微调也获得了更好的后训练性能，说明RL阶段的探索能力得到提升。

Conclusion: 在低数据条件下，SFT应优先激活任务相关能力而非记忆特定内容；EKSFT通过熵-KL选择性掩蔽策略，有效缓解了分布偏移问题，为后续RL阶段提供了更好的探索基础。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard post-training paradigm for large language models. This paradigm provides a cold-start for RL exploration, avoiding the inefficiency of pure RL where on-policy sampling yields insufficient positive samples. However, in practice, existing approaches often use a small amount of data for SFT initialization compared to the RL phase, which can cause the model to fit the limited samples and shift away from its pre-trained distribution. This distribution shift impedes the model's ability to effectively explore during subsequent RL training. To address this challenge, we propose that in low-data regimes, SFT should prioritize activating task-relevant capabilities rather than memorizing specific content. Along this line, we propose EKSFT (Entropy-KL Selective Fine-Tuning), which selectively masks tokens that exhibit either high entropy or high KL divergence from a reference model. By excluding these high-uncertainty, distribution-shifting tokens from imitation, EKSFT injects task-specific knowledge while preserving the integrity of the model's pre-trained distribution. Empirical evaluations on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that EKSFT consistently outperforms standard SFT. Further RL fine-tuning from the EKSFT model yields consistently better post-RL performance, indicating improved exploration for the RL stage. Our codes and datasets are available at https://github.com/MINE-USTC/EKSFT.

</details>


### [84] [PassNet: Scaling Large Language Models for Graph Compiler Pass Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29357)
*Yiqun Liu,Yingsheng Wu,Ruqi Yang,Enrong Zheng,Honglei Qiu,Sijun He,Tai Liang,Jingjing Wu,Yuhan Zhou,Yiwei Zhang,Dongyan Chen,Weihan Yi,Xinqi Li,Siqi Bao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PassNet是一个首个大规模LLM编译器Pass生成生态系统，包含PassNet-Dataset（18K+真实计算图）和PassBench（200个长尾融合任务，2,060个子图），使用错误感知加速分数（ES_t）评估。研究发现LLM在个别子图上可获得3倍加速，但整体落后TorchInductor 37%，表明瓶颈在于一致性而非能力；在4K轨迹上微调的小模型可实现2.67倍提升，验证PassNet作为LLM驱动编译器优化的实时训练基础设施的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代张量编译器（如TorchInductor）在长尾工作负载上面临系统性性能天花板，43%的真实子图在默认编译下反而变慢。现有LLM优化工作仅关注独立内核生成，而作者认为Pass生成（LLM生成结构化图变换并直接集成到编译器流水线）是更合适的抽象。

Method: 提出PassNet生态系统，包含：(1) PassNet-Dataset，超过18K个来自10万真实模型的独特计算图；(2) PassBench，200个经过筛选的长尾融合任务（共2,060个子图），使用错误感知加速分数（ES_t）评估，该指标统一了正确性、稳定性和性能，并采用分层完整性防御以防止系统性LLM滥用。

Result: PassBench既具有高度区分度又真正未饱和：最佳前沿模型整体落后TorchInductor 37%，但在个别子图上LLM可达到3倍加速；在仅4K PassNet轨迹上微调的小模型可获得2.67倍提升，接近前沿模型性能，表明瓶颈在于一致性而非能力，且LLM驱动编译器优化有巨大提升空间。

Conclusion: PassNet验证了LLM驱动编译器优化的巨大潜力，其数据集、基准和工具均已公开，可作为实时训练基础设施推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Modern tensor compilers such as TorchInductor deliver substantial speedups on mainstream models, yet face a systematic performance ceiling on long-tail workloads -- our profiling shows that 43% of real-world subgraphs experience end-to-end slowdowns under default compilation. While LLMs offer a path toward automated optimization, existing efforts focus on standalone kernel generation. We argue that pass generation -- where LLMs author structured graph transformations that integrate directly into compiler pipelines -- is the more appropriate abstraction. We propose PassNet, the first large-scale ecosystem for LLM-based compiler pass generation, comprising: (1) PassNet-Dataset, over 18K unique computational graphs from 100K real-world models; and (2) PassBench, 200 curated long-tail fusible tasks (comprising 2,060 subgraphs in total) evaluated under the Error-aware Speedup Score (ES_t) -- a metric unifying correctness, stability, and performance -- with layered integrity defenses against systematic LLM exploitation. Experiments reveal that PassBench is both highly discriminative and genuinely unsaturated: the best frontier model trails TorchInductor by 37% in aggregate, yet on individual subgraphs LLMs achieve up to 3x speedup over the same compiler -- indicating that the bottleneck is consistency, not capability. Fine-tuning a small model on merely ~4K PassNet trajectories yields a 2.67x improvement approaching frontier-model performance, demonstrating substantial headroom and validating PassNet as live training infrastructure for advancing LLM-driven compiler optimization. All data, benchmarks, and tooling are publicly available.

</details>


### [85] [Towards Human-Like Interactive Speech Recognition With Agentic Correction and Semantic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29430)
*Zixuan Jiang,Yanqiao Zhu,Peng Wang,Qinyuan Chen,Xinjian Zhao,Xipeng Qiu,Wupeng Wang,Zhifu Gao,Xiangang Li,Kai Yu,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agentic ASR框架，通过语义纠错、意图路由和基于推理的编辑，实现迭代式多轮语音识别优化；引入LLM驱动的S²ER指标和交互模拟系统，显著降低语义错误，优于传统指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ASR采用单次通过范式，与人类交流方式不一致，难以纠正语义关键错误；token级指标（如WER、CER）无法充分反映语义错误问题。

Method: 提出Agentic ASR框架，结合单次通过ASR前端与语义纠错、意图路由和基于推理的编辑；引入基于LLM的S²ER语义误差率和交互模拟系统。

Result: 多语言、命名实体密集和代码切换基准测试显示，迭代交互持续降低语义错误，S²ER收益显著大于传统token级指标；人类-AI对齐和消融实验验证了语义评估可靠性和框架鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 交互式ASR范式通过多轮细化显著提升语义准确性，S²ER作为更贴近人类理解的评估指标，为ASR评估和交互式系统设计提供新方向。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) is a core component of human--computer interaction and an increasingly important front-end for LLM-based assistants and agents. However, most current ASR systems still follow a single-pass paradigm, which is poorly aligned with human communication, where misunderstandings are resolved through iterative clarification and refinement. This mismatch makes it difficult to correct meaning-critical errors once they occur. Meanwhile, token-level metrics such as WER or CER cannot adequately reflect such a problem. To address these limitations, we formulate \emph{Interactive ASR} as a multi-turn refinement task and propose \textbf{Agentic ASR}, a closed-loop framework that combines a single-pass ASR front-end with semantic correction, intent routing, and reasoning-based editing. We further introduce the \textbf{Sentence-level Semantic Error Rate} ($S^2ER$), an LLM-based semantic evaluation metric, together with an \textbf{Interactive Simulation System} for scalable and reproducible benchmarking. Experiments on multilingual, named-entity-intensive, and code-switching benchmarks show that iterative interaction consistently reduces semantic errors, with much larger gains in $S^2ER$ than in conventional token-level metrics. Human--AI alignment and ablation studies further validate the reliability of the semantic judge and the robustness of the proposed framework. The code is available at: https://interactiveasr.github.io/ and the live demo is available at https://i-asr.sjtuxlance.com/

</details>


### [86] [The Curse of Helpfulness: Inverse Scaling Law in Robustness to Distractor Instructions via DistractionIF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29491)
*Zeli Su,Zhankai Xu,Tianlei Chen,Longfei Zheng,Xiaolu Zhang,Jun Zhou,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DistractionIF基准测试发现大模型在RAG场景中存在"逆向缩放"现象（模型越大越易受噪声干扰），GRPO强化学习可恢复模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在RAG系统中处理外部参考文本时，文本常包含编辑注释等良性但类指令的语义噪声，应严格视为数据而非指令，现有模型缺乏对此类干扰的鲁棒性评估基准。

Method: 1. 构建DistractionIF基准测试，评估模型处理含干扰指令参考文本的能力；2. 对比不同规模模型的性能表现；3. 通过困惑度分析揭示缩放如何模糊鲁棒行为与干扰行为的概率边界；4. 使用GRPO强化学习方法训练模型以恢复鲁棒性边界。

Result: 1. 发现"逆向缩放"现象：模型规模越大，鲁棒性越差，性能下降最高达30点；2. 困惑度分析表明缩放使模型更倾向于将噪声误判为指令；3. GRPO强化学习可恢复概率边界，提升鲁棒性最高15.5%，且不损害通用指令遵循能力。

Conclusion: 揭示参考文本驱动任务中的关键指令遵循鲁棒性缺口，证明强化学习是实现大规模数据与指令严格分离的有效路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in agentic and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems, where they must execute user-specified tasks over externally provided reference text. In practice, such context is often unstructured and contaminated with benign but instruction-like semantic noise, such as editorial comments and system traces, which should be treated strictly as data. We introduce DistractionIF, a benchmark designed to evaluate robustness against such distractor instructions in reference text. Across a broad range of models, we observe a consistent inverse scaling phenomenon: larger models are often less robust, with performance dropping by up to 30 points as scale increases. Mechanistically, our perplexity analysis reveals that scaling erodes the probabilistic boundary between robust and distracted behaviors, making models increasingly prone to over-interpreting noise as instructions. To address this, we demonstrate that reinforcement learning, specifically Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), can restore this boundary, improving robustness by up to 15.5% without compromising general instruction-following capability. Our findings highlight a critical instruction-following robustness gap in reference-grounded tasks and establish reinforcement learning as a promising path for enforcing strict data-instruction separation at scale.

</details>


### [87] [MINDGAMES: A Live Arena for Evaluating Social and Strategic Reasoning in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29512)
*Kevin Wang,Anna Thöni,Benjamin Kempinski,Bobby Cheng,Jianzhu Yao,Benjamin Finch,Leon Guertler,Viraj Nadkarni,Yihan Jiang,Aliaksei Korshuk,Alexander Buyantuev,Ilya Makarov,Siyuan Wu,Yu-Chi Cheng,Yan-Ru Ju,Ti-Rong Wu,I-Hsuan Chu,Yu-Yu Yang,I-Chen Wu,Yitian Huang,Qinlu Cao,Yiheng Sun,Yuhong Dai,Hongkun Yao,Jingxuan Fu,Jiwei Zhang,Hao Liao,Mossimo Ebeling,Govind Arun,Sadhvik Bathini,Mihir S Arya,Avinash Anish,Aditya Ranjan,Kirtana Sunil Phatnani,Paval KS,Vrushali Mehta,Aravind S,Nikhil Arora,Tanya Upadhyay,Amol Bandagale,Yuan Lu,ChunEn Hsiao,YuTing Lin,Arvin Chung,Jerry John Thomas,Mathieu Laurière,Leshem Choshen,Yoram Bachrach,Pramod Viswanath,Maria Polukarov,Cheston Tan,Tal Kachman,Atlas Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mindgames是一个面向大语言模型的多游戏竞技场和评估平台，用于测试LLM在多轮交互中的社会推理和策略能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为交互式智能体的部署日益普及，但其在长期交互中的社会推理和策略能力仍缺乏充分理解。现有评估方法依赖静态场景或单游戏基准，无法捕捉真实多智能体设置所需的持续、多维推理。

Method: 构建Mindgames平台，基于TextArena提供统一交互界面、基于TrueSkill的评分系统和完整轨迹记录。平台包含四个游戏环境：Colonel Blotto、重复囚徒困境、Codenames和Secret Mafia。引入MG-Ref确定性离线锦标赛协议用于评估。

Result: 2025年竞赛周期评估了来自76个团队的944个智能体。分析显示：规则遵循脆弱性仍是主要瓶颈；顶级系统依赖显式结构化辅助；不同环境的排行榜有效性差异显著；Secret Mafia游戏存在错误生存混淆效应。

Conclusion: 多智能体评估需要持续、多维的推理能力测试。Mindgames揭示了当前LLM智能体的局限性，包括对结构化辅助的依赖和规则遵循的不稳定性，同时强调了错误生存混淆等评估设计挑战。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as interactive agents, yet their capacity for social and strategic reasoning over extended interaction remains poorly understood. Existing evaluations rely on static vignettes or single-game benchmarks that cannot capture the sustained, multi-faceted reasoning that real-world multi-agent settings demand. We introduce Mindgames, a multi-game arena and evaluation platform for LLM agents that operationalizes complementary reasoning demands relevant to ``theory of mind'': belief attribution under hidden information, opponent modeling through repeated strategic interaction, cooperative inference under knowledge asymmetries, and sustained deception in social deduction. Built on TextArena, Mindgames provides a unified interaction interface, TrueSkill-based rating, and full trajectory logging across four game environments. We instantiate Mindgames through a 2025 competition cycle hosted at a major AI conference, which assessed 944 submitted agents from 76 teams across four games: Colonel Blotto, Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma, Codenames, and Secret Mafia. Our analysis surfaces both agent-level and evaluation-level limitations: brittle rule adherence remains a major bottleneck, top-performing systems repeatedly rely on explicit structural scaffolding, and leaderboard validity differs sharply across environments. In particular, failure-heavy environments can reward robustness to opponent errors as much as strategic ability, with Secret Mafia exhibiting a pronounced error-survival confound in this cycle. We release a dataset of 29,571 multi-agent games with turn-level observations, actions, and rewards, together with MG-Ref, a deterministic offline tournament protocol that scores new agents against a frozen reference pool of top-ranked, low-error Stage~II submissions under the same error-attribution lens used in this analysis.

</details>


### [88] [DeepTool: Scaling Interleaved Deliberation in Tool-Integrated Reasoning via Process-Supervised Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29568)
*Yang He,Xiao Ding,Bibo Cai,Yufei Zhang,Kai Xiong,Zhouhao Sun,Bing Qin,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepTool提出一种框架，通过在思考-行动-观察交错过程中扩展深思熟虑的思考，结合合成管道和基于GRPO的过程监督强化学习，解决TIR中缺乏顺序工具调用的深思熟虑和自我纠正问题，显著提升模型性能并保持token成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TIR方法缺乏顺序工具调用时的深思熟虑和自我纠正能力；传统强化学习方法受限于稀疏的结果奖励，无法监督中间推理步骤和工具调用，导致无法有效指导模型进行战略规划和自我修正。

Method: 1. 合成管道：将扩展思考演化为交错轨迹，集成对抗性扰动确保鲁棒性和自我纠正；2. 基于GRPO的过程监督强化学习：使用行动中心的过程奖励强化中间交错思考和确保精确工具调用。

Result: 在六个基准测试中显著提升Qwen2.5-7B性能（AIME24: 3.2% → 40.4%，HMMT25: 0.0% → 28.6%），token成本效益分析证实交错思考的效用，实现性能与token效率的最佳平衡。

Conclusion: DeepTool成功解决了TIR中战略规划和自我纠正的缺失问题，通过过程监督强化学习有效监督中间推理步骤，在提升性能的同时保持token成本效益，为复杂任务推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) extends LLM capabilities by leveraging external environments. However, existing methods lack the deliberation during sequential tool invocation required for strategic planning and self-correction. While RL mitigates this, conventional approaches for Tool-Integrated Reasoning are hindered by sparse outcome-based rewards, failing to supervise intermediate reasoning steps and tool invocations. To address this, we propose DeepTool, a novel framework that scales deliberate thinking within the interleaved process of thinking, action, and observation at each turn. In DeepTool, we first introduce a synthesis pipeline that evolves extended thinking into interleaved trajectories, integrating adversarial perturbations to ensure robustness and self-correction. Secondly, we devise Process-Supervised Reinforcement Learning based on GRPO, which utilizes an Action-Centric Process Reward to reinforce intermediate interleaved thinking and enforce precise tool invocation at every turn. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeepTool achieves superior performance, boosting Qwen2.5-7B significantly across six benchmarks (e.g., AIME24: 3.2% -> 40.4% and HMMT25: 0.0% -> 28.6%). Furthermore, the token cost-effectiveness analysis confirms the utility of interleaved thinking, demonstrating DeepTool's optimal balance between performance and token efficiency.

</details>


### [89] [GPS-Enhanced Tourist Mobility Modeling with Seasonal Spatial Priors and LLM-Based Activity Chain Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29578)
*Yifan Liu,Yanling Sang,Xishun Liao,Morgan Sun,Bo Yang,Zhiyuan Zhang,Chris Stanford,Haoxuan Ma,Jiaqi Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个四阶段仿真框架，结合GPS聚合数据、人口统计特征和LLM生成技术，构建隐私保护且符合人口统计特征的游客出行模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游客出行具有非程式化、目的地驱动、对出行目的、季节和出行成员构成高度敏感等特点，现有方法要么仅测量聚合空间模式而不生成个人行程，要么合成出行但缺乏游客特定的结构（如行程时长条件、月度需求变化、家庭共行规则）

Method: 四阶段仿真框架：1) 从GPS和调查数据提取月份条件空间先验；2) 从游客人口统计特征预测行程范围；3) 基于距离可行性进行社区序列分配；4) 在家庭和空间约束下使用LLM生成活动链。GPS数据仅以隐私保护的聚合形式使用

Result: 在东京旅游实验中，GPS提取的游客群体恢复的空间访问签名与调查参考一致；框架生成的符合人口统计的合成行程，其社区级访问份额与调查分布及停留点导出的月度访问模式紧密对齐

Conclusion: 该框架作为地理基础、人口统计感知的游客出行建模方法，证明了其有效性，能够在不泄露个体轨迹的情况下生成符合真实模式的出行模拟数据

Abstract: Tourist mobility poses a distinct challenge for urban transportation planning. Unlike resident commuting, tourist travel is largely non-routine, attraction driven, and highly sensitive to trip purpose, travel season, and trip member composition. Existing approaches either measure aggregate tourist spatial patterns without generating individual schedules, or synthesize mobility without tourist specific structure such as trip duration conditioning, month varying attraction demand, and household co-travel rules. To address these challenges, we propose a four stage simulation framework combining month conditioned spatial priors derived from GPS and survey data, trip extent prediction from tourist demographics, distance feasible ward sequence assignment, and LLM-based activity chain generation under household and spatial constraints. GPS data are used only in privacy preserving aggregated form as month conditioned spatial priors, with no individual traces retained or exposed. Experiments on tourism in Tokyo demonstrate that the GPS based tourist cohort extraction recovers spatial visitation signatures consistent with survey references, and our framework produces demographically aligned synthetic schedules whose ward-level visitation shares align closely with both survey distributions and staypoint derived monthly visitation patterns. The results demonstrate the framework's effectiveness as a geographically grounded, demographically aware approach to tourist mobility modeling.

</details>


### [90] [FinVerBench: Benchmark Validity and Calibration in Large Language Model Financial Statement Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29586)
*Silu Panda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinVerBench是一个金融报表验证基准测试，基于43家标普500公司的SEC 10-K XBRL文件，通过四类错误分类法评估LLM在检测数值一致性方面的能力。研究发现LLM在原始提示下对干净报表产生大量假阳性，且渲染方式显著影响召回率测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融报表验证是LLM在金融领域的关键能力，但缺乏可靠的基准测试。现有评估方法可能受提示设计和数值渲染方式影响，无法准确反映模型的真实能力。

Method: 构建包含43家标普500公司SEC 10-K XBRL文件的FinVerBench，定义算术、跨报表链接、同比、幅度扰动四类错误分类法。对14个LLM进行评估，设置105个实例（43干净，62错误注入），对比原始未圆整和现实圆整两种渲染方式。

Result: 14个LLM中9个在原始提示下对干净报表产生95-100%假阳性。渲染方式显著影响召回率：未圆整版本100%召回率，圆整版本仅79.0%召回率。结论是财务报表验证是校准判断而非简单算术检测。

Conclusion: FinVerBench支持构念效度而非最终排行榜：财务报表验证是校准判断，受不完全可观察性、提示诱导假设和现实数值渲染影响。基准测试发布为未来研究提供公开数据集和代码。

Abstract: We introduce FinVerBench, a benchmark and validity study for financial statement verification: determining whether a set of corporate financial statements is numerically consistent from the information shown to the model. FinVerBench is built from SEC 10-K XBRL filings for 43 S&P 500 companies and defines a four-category error taxonomy covering arithmetic, cross-statement linkage, year-over-year, and magnitude perturbations. We attempt fifteen contemporary LLM evaluations and report fourteen complete runs; a Gemini 2.5 Pro run is excluded from the main comparison because 40/108 gateway calls failed. All binary metrics exclude underdetermined positive instances whose perturbed line item is not rendered, leaving a 105-instance observable diagnostic subset (43 clean, 62 error-injected). Under the original guided-checklist prompt on the unrounded diagnostic subset, nine of fourteen complete LLM runs produce 95-100% false positives on clean statements, while one run achieves 0% observed false positives. Benchmark rendering choices materially affect measured recall: on a realistic rounded variant of the same observable subset, the calibrated model's recall is 79.0% with 0% observed FPR, compared with 100.0% recall on the unrounded diagnostic variant. These results support a construct-validity conclusion rather than a final leaderboard: financial statement verification is not merely arithmetic detection, but calibrated judgment under incomplete observability, prompt-induced assumptions, and realistic numerical rendering. FinVerBench and all code are publicly available.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond Attack Success Rate: Temporal Logit Observability for LLM Safety Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29629)
*Junyoung Park,Sunghwan Park,Seongyong Ju,Jaewoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种训练无关的诊断方法Temporal Logit Observability (TLO)，利用解码过程中的对齐-拒绝边缘来可视化攻击的隐藏路径，弥补了Attack Success Rate (ASR)只能判断失败与否但无法揭示失败过程的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR仅用是/否标签评估攻击成功率，无法揭示攻击路径和失败机制。两个产生同等有害输出的攻击可能遵循完全不同的路径，而ASR无法区分它们。

Method: TLO是一种训练无关的诊断方法，通过监控解码过程中的合规性-拒绝边缘，将每个模型-攻击条件放置在校准的2D平面上。该方法仅使用logits（无需隐藏状态），通过早期停止规则实现更有效的攻击拦截。

Result: 在4个对齐LLM和3种越狱范式上，ASR相近的攻击在TLO平面上落在不同位置，揭示了同一模型可通过不同时间模式失败。几何结构与拒绝方向探针匹配，早期停止规则使成功攻击减少一半以上且对良性查询无误报。

Conclusion: 安全评估应报告失败何时以及如何发生，而不仅仅是是否发生。TLO使这两种信息仅通过logits即可观察，为安全评估提供了比ASR更具信息量的诊断工具。

Abstract: Attack Success Rate (ASR) evaluates each jailbreak with a single yes/no label at the end of generation, telling us whether a failure happened but not how it unfolded. Two attacks that produce equally harmful outputs may have followed completely different paths, and ASR cannot tell them apart. We make those hidden paths observable from logits alone. Temporal Logit Observability (TLO) is a training-free diagnostic that watches a compliance-refusal margin during decoding and places each model-attack condition on a calibrated 2D plane. By design, this plane is most informative exactly where ASR is least informative: among attacks that succeed for genuinely different reasons. Across four aligned LLMs and three jailbreak paradigms, attacks with nearly identical ASR land at clearly different points on the plane: the same model can fail through different temporal patterns. The geometry matches refusal-direction probes from hidden states on most conditions, with one model showing the limit of our fixed-lexicon approach. A simple early-stop rule derived from TLO cuts successful jailbreaks by more than half, without false alarms on plain benign queries. Safety evaluation should report when and how a failure unfolds, not only whether it occurred. TLO makes the first two observable from logits alone.

</details>


### [92] [VikingMem: A Memory Base Management System for Stateful LLM-based Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29640)
*Jiajie Fu,Junwen Chen,Mengzhao Wang,Aoxiang He,Maojia Sheng,Xiangyu Ke,Yifan Zhu,Yunjun Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VikingMem系统，通过Memory Base范式实现LLM长期状态管理，采用事件中心提取、实体动态演化和时间加权压缩等技术，在长期记忆检索基准测试中相比基线提升30%效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的有限上下文窗口限制了其维护长期、有状态交互的能力。现有内存方法依赖简单提取导致记忆不完整，或使用针对单一用例（如聊天机器人）的刚性提示，缺乏泛化性和跨任务适应性。

Method: 提出Memory Base新范式，包含三个核心原则：从原始信息流中选择性提取高价值记忆、具有状态性和演化性（记忆内容渐进式总结、修正和时间加权）、通用化抽象范式。基于此构建VikingMem系统，使用VikingDB向量引擎，通过事件和实体抽象实现事件中心提取、动态更新实体、主题时间线压缩和时间加权召回。

Result: 在长期记忆基准测试中，VikingMem在记忆检索有效性方面相比基线提升高达30%，同时保持了交互应用所需的低延迟。

Conclusion: VikingMem通过Memory Base范式为LLM长期交互状态管理提供了鲁棒解决方案，具有跨教育、推荐和智能体记忆等多样化应用的通用性。

Abstract: Large Language Models have revolutionized interactive applications; however, their finite context windows pose a critical data management challenge for maintaining stateful, long-term interactions. Existing memory approaches often rely on simplistic extraction methods that lead to incomplete memories or use rigid, single-purpose memory extraction prompts tailored to a single use case, such as chatbots. Consequently, they lack generalizability and perform poorly across diverse downstream tasks. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Memory Base, a novel data management paradigm for managing the persistent state of long-term interactions. It is characterized by three core principles: selective extraction of high-value memories from raw information streams; inherent statefulness and evolution, where memory content is progressively summarized, corrected, and temporally weighted to prioritize recent interactions; and a generalizable abstraction paradigm designed for robust transferability across diverse applications, including education, recommendation, and agent memory. Building on this foundation, we present VikingMem, an end-to-end Memory Base Management System implemented on the VikingDB vector engine. VikingMem materializes this paradigm through interconnected event and entity abstractions. It features event-centric memory extraction to selectively handle complex information streams, while entities are dynamically updated by events to achieve stateful evolution. Using temporal compression via a topic-wise timeline and time-weighted recall, the system progressively produces high-level summary memories, prioritizes recent items, and compresses and fades older ones. Extensive evaluations on long-term memory benchmarks demonstrate that VikingMem outperformes baselines by up to 30% in memory retrieval effectiveness while maintaining the low latency essential for interactive applications.

</details>


### [93] [LLM-Evolved Domain-Independent Heuristics for Symbolic AI Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29649)
*Elliot Gestrin,Jendrik Seipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过进化搜索与LLM结合，首次生成了超越人工设计最佳启发式算法的通用规划启发式，在未见过的测试领域上表现优于最强基线，且整个启发式函数集覆盖了性能帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 启发式搜索是符号AI规划的主导范式，最强的启发式算法经过了数十年的研究。虽然近期工作表明LLM可以为单个规划领域设计启发式，但尚未有LLM生成的启发式能够适用于任意规划任务。因此需要能够泛化到任意规划任务的领域无关启发式算法。

Method: 采用进化搜索方法生成LLM生成的领域无关启发式算法：让LLM对用C++编写的父启发式进行变异，将候选程序存储在以“信息度”和“速度”为键的MAP-Elites存档中，通过融合覆盖率与求解时间来计算适应度分数。同时，对一组手工程启发式算法在信息度-速度权衡上进行基准测试。

Result: 在未见过的测试领域上，最佳进化启发式算法解决的任务数量超过最强基线；整个启发式函数集覆盖了信息度-速度权衡的帕累托前沿。实验发现，从盲启发式开始进化比从FF启发式开始进化效果更好，即使最终生成的程序本身就是FF的变体；LLM推理努力影响候选程序能否通过编译的频率，远大于影响已编译程序的质量。

Conclusion: 进化生成的程序是纯C++代码，可作为即插即用的替换方案集成到现有规划器中，并继承底层搜索的完备性和正确性保证。这表明进化搜索结合LLM可以产生优于人工设计的通用规划启发式算法。

Abstract: Heuristic search is the dominant paradigm in symbolic AI planning, and the strongest heuristics are the result of decades of work by planning researchers. Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can design heuristics for individual planning domains, but no LLM-generated heuristic has so far worked on arbitrary planning tasks. In this paper, we use evolutionary search to produce the first LLM-generated domain-independent heuristics that exceed the hand-engineered state of the art. We let an LLM mutate parent heuristics written in C++, store candidates in a MAP-Elites archive keyed on informedness and speed and calculate fitness scores by blending coverage with solving time. To place the evolved programs in context, we additionally benchmark a broad set of hand-engineered heuristics on their informedness-speed tradeoff, which to our knowledge has not been done before. On unseen testing domains, our best evolved heuristic solves more tasks than even the strongest baseline, with our full heuristic suite spanning the Pareto frontier of said tradeoff. We also find that seeding evolution from the trivial blind heuristic outperforms seeding from the strong FF heuristic, even when the resulting program is itself an FF variant, and that LLM reasoning effort affects how often candidates compile much more than the quality of those that do. Because the evolved programs are plain C++, they slot into existing planners as drop-in replacements and inherit the soundness and completeness guarantees of the underlying search.

</details>


### [94] [TRACE: Toulmin-based Reasoning Assessment through Constructive Elements for LLM CoT Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29656)
*Yundong Kim,Heyoung Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为TRACE的新指标，用于评估大语言模型的链式思维推理过程。TRACE通过整合图尔敏论证理论和弗拉维尔元认知框架，分析论证的结构而非仅评判最终答案。在26.3K个QA样本上的实验显示，TRACE与基准准确率的相关性高达0.74，且能作为强化学习的有效奖励信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型（LLM）开放式输出仍然具有挑战性，因为缺乏真实答案。现有指标主要依赖最终答案的准确性或表面统计信息，导致推理过程本身未被考察。研究旨在解决这一评估空白，提供更全面的推理质量评估方法。

Method: TRACE通过整合图尔敏论证理论和弗拉维尔元认知框架，分析链式思维（CoT）推理过程中的论证构建方式，而非仅评判最终结果。该方法评估推理的结构性和逻辑性，将论证要素（如主张、证据、限定条件等）纳入评估体系。

Result: 在7个推理模型和26.3K个QA样本上的实验表明，TRACE与基准准确率的相关性为0.74，显示出强相关性。此外，TRACE作为强化学习的奖励信号时，性能优于仅基于准确率的基线方法。

Conclusion: 逻辑合理的推理能够产生更高质量的答案。TRACE作为LLM开放式输出的互补评估指标，能够有效衡量推理质量，为模型优化提供有价值的指导。

Abstract: Evaluating open-ended outputs from large language models (LLMs) remains challenging due to the absence of ground truth. Existing metrics rely on final-answer accuracy or surface-level statistics, leaving the reasoning process itself unexamined. We introduce TRACE (Toulmin-based Reasoning Assessment through Constructive Elements), a metric that analyzes Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning processes. Rather than judging outcomes, TRACE inspects how arguments are constructed by integrating Toulmin's argumentation theory with Flavell's metacognitive framework to assess reasoning structure. Experiments on 26.3K QA samples across 7 reasoning models show strong correlation with benchmark accuracy (r=0.74). Furthermore, TRACE is effective as a reinforcement learning reward signal, outperforming accuracy-only baselines. Together, these results indicate that logically sound reasoning leads to higher-quality answers. TRACE thus serves as a complementary metric for evaluating open-ended outputs. Code is available at https://github.com/hyyangkisti/trace.

</details>


### [95] [Reliable Reasoning with Large Language Models via Preference-Based Maximum Satisfiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29687)
*Pedro Orvalho,Marta Kwiatkowska,Guillem Alenyà,Felip Manyà*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大语言模型在处理多约束优化任务时存在困难，本文提出通过代码生成将自然语言问题转化为偏好最大可满足性问题（MaxSAT），利用精确求解器求解并通过独立验证确保正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs擅长自然语言理解，但在机器人等领域的多约束和用户偏好优化任务中表现不佳，现有直接回答、思维链和程序思维方法很少产生可行解。

Method: 提出混合推理方法：1) LLM生成Python代码将用户约束和偏好编码为偏好MaxSAT问题；2) 使用精确MaxSAT求解器求解；3) 通过与标准MaxSAT编码的独立验证确保解的可行性和最优性。

Result: 在三个偏好推理任务上评估，MaxSAT管道接受率显著高于基线（有时超过80%），而基线很少产生可行解。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的代码生成结合偏好MaxSAT可实现可验证的优化，显著提高了在独立验证参考语义下的正确性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at understanding natural language but struggle with optimisation tasks involving multiple constraints and user-defined preferences, which commonly arise in domains such as robotics. We propose a hybrid reasoning approach in which LLMs externalise reasoning through code generation. Given a natural language problem description, an LLM generates Python code that encodes user-defined constraints and preferences as a preference-based Maximum Satisfiability (MaxSAT) problem, which is then solved by an exact MaxSAT solver. To ensure correctness, solutions returned by the model-generated code are independently verified for feasibility and optimality against a canonical MaxSAT encoding, allowing for different encodings and multiple optimal solutions. We evaluate our approach using both open-source and closed-access LLMs on three families of preference-based reasoning tasks, and compare it against direct-answer, chain-of-thought, and program-of-thought baselines using the same models. While these baselines rarely produce feasible solutions, the MaxSAT-based pipeline achieves substantially higher acceptance rates, in some cases exceeding 80%. Our results demonstrate that LLM-driven code generation combined with preference-based MaxSAT enables solver-verifiable optimisation with respect to generated encodings, and substantially improves correctness under independently verified reference semantics.

</details>


### [96] [BitTP: The Lightweight Trajectory Prediction Model with BitLLM for Edge-Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29705)
*Mincheol Kang,Hyunjin Lim,Bomin Kang,Daehee Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BitTP是一种将基于LLM的轨迹预测器转换为轻量级bit线性架构的方法，通过1.58位权重仅量化（激活值保持全精度）实现边缘设备上的高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹预测对自主系统至关重要，需要复杂的智能体交互推理。虽然LLM提供了强大的上下文推理和可解释的基于语言的轨迹表示，但其极其占用内存和计算资源，难以部署在资源受限的边缘设备上（如自主机器人的车载计算机）。

Method: 提出BitTP方法，将基于LLM的轨迹预测器转换为轻量级bit线性架构。研究发现仅对权重进行1.58位量化（BitTP-Weight）是最优的，而激活值必须保持全精度，否则会导致时空推理的严重退化和不稳定。

Result: BitTP-Weight不仅保持了甚至提高了预测质量，相比全精度BF16基线平均降低ADE 14.29%、FDE 20.97%，同时减少了内存使用和推理延迟。这些结果表明精心设计的量化作为有效的正则化器，使得在边缘设备上实际部署复杂的LLM推理成为可能。

Conclusion: 精心设计的量化方法（权重仅量化至1.58位，激活值保持全精度）作为有效的正则化器，能够实现复杂LLM推理在边缘设备上的实用化部署，平衡了性能与资源效率。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction is a fundamental task for autonomous systems, requiring complex reasoning about multi-agent interactions and intents. Large language models (LLMs) have recently been adopted for this task, as they provide strong contextual reasoning and interpretable, language-based trajectory representations. However, these LLM-based predictors are extremely memory- and compute-intensive, making them difficult to deploy on resource-constrained edge devices such as on-board computers in autonomous robots. To bridge this gap, we propose BitTP, which converts an LLM-based trajectory predictor into a lightweight bitlinear architecture. We demonstrate that weight-only quantization to 1.58-bit (BitTP-Weight) is optimal. Crucially, activations must remain in full precision, as quantizing them leads to severe degradation and instability in spatio-temporal reasoning. Empirically, BitTP-Weight not only preserves but improves prediction quality over the full-precision (BF16) LLM baseline, reducing ADE by 14.29% and FDE by 20.97% on average, while simultaneously reducing memory usage and inference latency relative to other quantization methods. These results demonstrate that carefully designed quantization acts as an effective regularizer, enabling the practical deployment of sophisticated LLM-based reasoning on edge devices. Code is available at: https://github.com/MintCat98/BitTP.

</details>


### [97] [Citation-Closure Retrieval and Per-Rule Attribution for Real-World Regulatory Compliance Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29742)
*Yeong-Joon Ju,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RegOps-Bench基准测试和RefWalk框架，用于解决大语言模型在监管合规问答任务中的引用追踪和证据归因问题，通过跨文档引用遍历和多视图候选融合，显著提升了检索召回率和引用准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署LLM进行监管合规需要通过多层权威结构进行严格的可追溯引用。现有RAG系统在处理复杂引用关系时存在引用边扁平化、检索扩展碎片化和事后归因脆弱等问题，无法满足监管合规任务对结构化程序查询和证据集闭合的要求。

Method: 1. 提出RegOps-Bench基准：基于复杂国家研发法规构建操作知识图谱；2. 设计RefWalk框架：以共享主题锚点驱动，遍历跨文档引用，通过基于最大值的聚合融合多视图候选，强制执行每条规则的归因以明确映射声明到来源。

Result: 在RegOps-Bench上建立了强基线，显著提高了检索召回率和引用准确率。在HIPAA健康合规数据集上的对比评估显示，现有系统在扁平结构规则上已达到饱和，凸显了RegOps-Bench的必要性。

Conclusion: RegOps-Bench和RefWalk为监管合规QA任务提供了有效的评估框架和解决方案，现有系统在处理复杂多层引用结构时存在明显局限，需要新的基准来推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) for regulatory compliance demands rigorous traceability via comprehensive citations across multi-tiered authority structures. Unlike traditional multi-hop or legal QA, this task requires structured procedural lookups and evidence-set closure rather than entity resolution or case-law reasoning. Existing RAG systems struggle here due to flattened citation edges, fragmented retrieval expansions, and fragile post-hoc attribution. We formalize Regulatory Compliance QA with RegOps-Bench, a novel benchmark featuring an Operational Knowledge Graph derived from complex national R\&D regulations. To address these bottlenecks, we propose RefWalk, a unified framework driven by a shared topic anchor. RefWalk traverses cross-document citations, fuses multi-view candidates via max-based aggregation, and enforces per-rule attribution to explicitly map claims to sources. We establish a strong baseline with substantial improvements in retrieval recall and citation accuracy. Finally, a contrastive evaluation on a U.S. health compliance dataset (HIPAA) reveals that existing systems exhibit saturation on flat-structure rules, underscoring the need for RegOps-Bench. Our code is available at https://github.com/yeongjoonJu/RefWalk.

</details>


### [98] [Why Specialist Models Still Matter: A Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Paradigm for Medical Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29744)
*Yanan Wang,Shuaicong Hu,Jian Liu,Guohui Zhou,Aiguo Wang,Cuiwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HetMedAgent是一个异构医疗多智能体框架，通过冲突感知的证据融合、基于不确定性的临床医生干预触发和自适应阈值校准，使通用大模型与领域专用专家模型协同工作，在临床决策任务中显著优于单一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在医疗领域的出色表现引发了一个关键问题：领域专用的医疗专家模型是否会被淘汰？作者认为医疗AI的未来不在于构建单一的医疗基础模型或替代人类专家，而在于协调通用大模型、领域专用专家模型和临床医生之间的协作。

Method: HetMedAgent框架包含三大核心机制：1）冲突感知的证据融合，用于整合不同模型的观点；2）基于不确定性的临床医生干预触发，在模型不确定时请求专家介入；3）自适应阈值校准，根据任务需求动态调整判断标准。

Result: 在三个真实世界的临床决策任务上，通用大模型与领域专用专家模型的协同表现显著优于单独使用任一类型的模型，验证了专家模型在特定模态分析中的不可替代价值。

Conclusion: 该研究代表了从构建医疗大模型或基础模型向多智能体协作范式的转变，实现了通用推理能力与领域特定精度之间的平衡，强调了多智能体协作在医疗AI中的重要性。

Abstract: The impressive performance of generalist large language models (LLMs) such as GPT and Claude in healthcare raises a critical question: will domain-specific medical specialist models become obsolete? We argue that the future of medical artificial intelligence (AI) lies not in building monolithic medical foundation models, nor in replacing human expertise, but in orchestrating collaboration among generalist LLMs, domain-specific specialist models, and clinicians. We propose HetMedAgent, a heterogeneous medical multi-agent framework that enables conflict-aware evidence fusion, uncertainty-based clinician intervention triggering, and adaptive threshold calibration. Experiments on three real-world clinical decision-making tasks demonstrate that the synergy between generalist LLMs and domain-specific specialist models significantly outperforms using either type of model alone, validating the irreplaceable value of specialist models in modality-specific analysis. HetMedAgent represents a shift from building medical LLMs or foundation models to multi-agent collaboration, achieving a balance between general reasoning capabilities and domain-specific precision.

</details>


### [99] [OptSkills: Learning Generalizable Optimization Skills from Problem Archetypes via Cluster-Based Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29829)
*Haochen Yang,Ke Zhao,Mengyuan Ma,Xingyu Lu,Xiangfeng Wang,Hong Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OptSkills是一个基于原型中心技能学习和推理的系统，通过按原型聚类问题、探索多样化建模范式、蒸馏成功轨迹为可重用技能，以及扩展技能库来提升优化问题建模和求解的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法在自动构建和求解优化问题时存在局限性：对表面叙述变化敏感、主要在案例层面复用经验、难以适应转移或新兴问题类型，导致泛化能力不足。

Method: 1. 原型聚类：按问题原型而非表面叙述聚类问题，提高鲁棒泛化；2. 多样化探索：在每簇内探索不同建模范式和求解器配置；3. 技能蒸馏：将成功轨迹蒸馏为可重用的工作流级技能；4. 技能扩展：利用新轨迹细化现有技能或扩展技能库。

Result: 在包含多种问题类型和场景的数据集上达到68.27%的微平均准确率；在MIPLIB-NL上达到26.91%，超过DeepSeek-V3.2-Thinking 4.53%；在Nano-CO上学习后，在OOD NLCO基准上达到72.79%。

Conclusion: 通过原型聚类、技能蒸馏和技能扩展机制，OptSkills显著提升了优化问题建模和求解的泛化能力，包括分布内和分布外的泛化，在多个基准上取得了优于现有方法的结果。

Abstract: Leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to automatically formulate and solve optimization problems from natural language has emerged as an efficient paradigm for automated optimization. However, existing methods still exhibit limited generalization: they are sensitive to superficial narrative variations, reuse experience mainly at the case level, and struggle to adapt to shifted or emerging problem types. We propose OptSkills, an archetype-centric skill learning and reasoning agent system for optimization modeling and solving. To improve robust generalization, our system clusters problems by their underlying archetypes rather than surface narratives. To improve in-distribution generalization, it explores diverse modeling paradigms and solver configurations within each cluster, then distills successful trajectories into reusable workflow-level skills. To improve out-of-distribution generalization, it refines existing skills or expands the skill library using newly obtained trajectories. Our system achieves a state-of-the-art micro-averaged accuracy of 68.27% on datasets encompassing diverse problem types and scenarios. In addition, on MIPLIB-NL, a highly challenging large-scale and high-dimensional benchmark, it achieves 26.91% accuracy, outperforming DeepSeek-V3.2-Thinking by 4.53%. After skill learning on Nano-CO, it reaches 72.79% on the OOD NLCO benchmark. Code and skills are available at https://github.com/fujiwaranoM0kou/OptSkills.

</details>


### [100] [OmniMatBench: A Human-Calibrated Multimodal Reasoning Benchmark Across 19 Materials Science Subfields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29833)
*Wanhao Liu,Jiaqing Xie,Qian Tan,Weida Wang,Jue Wang,Ran Sun,Zhuo Yang,Wanli Ouyang,Lei Bai,Tianfan Fu,Lu Chen,Xin Chen,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniMatBench是一个针对材料科学的多模态推理基准测试，包含3,171个专家编写的跨19个材料科学子领域的问答和计算问题，用于评估多模态语言模型在材料科学推理方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材料科学基准主要关注属性预测、知识问答或表征理解，忽略了从材料知识到应用的更广泛推理过程，导致该领域推理能力评估不足。

Method: 构建了OmniMatBench基准测试，包含3,171个专家编写的问答和计算问题，覆盖19个材料科学子领域，涵盖基础材料知识、结构工程材料、材料加工制造、功能应用材料四个维度。

Result: 评估了13个开源和闭源多模态大模型，最佳模型仅达到0.372的整体得分，揭示当前模型在材料科学推理方面存在显著差距。进一步分析显示模型在不同子领域表现差异大、推理启发式固定、材料知识不均衡、高级知识应用受限。

Conclusion: OmniMatBench提供了对当前多模态大模型能力和局限性的重要洞察，为材料科学研究中的可靠AI助手奠定了基础。

Abstract: As multimodal language models play an increasingly important role in scientific research, materials science offers a critical testbed due to its interdisciplinary, multimodal, and application-driven nature. However, existing materials benchmarks mainly focus on property prediction, knowledge QA, or characterization understanding, leaving the broader reasoning process from materials knowledge to application underexplored. To fill this gap, we present OmniMatBench, a human-calibrated multimodal reasoning benchmark for materials science. OmniMatBench contains 3,171 expert-curated QA and calculation problems across 19 materials-science subfields, spanning fundamental materials knowledge, structural and engineering materials, materials processing and manufacturing, and functional and applied materials. We evaluate 13 open-source and closed-source MLLMs and find that the best model achieves only a 0.372 overall score, revealing a substantial gap in current materials-science reasoning. Further analysis shows strong variation across subfields, fixed reasoning heuristics, uneven materials knowledge, and limited high-level knowledge application under formula-, retrieval-, and code-assisted settings. OmniMatBench provides crucial insights into the capabilities and limitations of current MLLMs and establishes a foundation for reliable AI assistants in materials-science research.

</details>


### [101] [Moment-KV: Momentum-Based Decode-Time KV Cache Compression for Long Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29873)
*Soumyadeep Jana,Sagar Nishad,Sanasam Ranbir Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Moment-KV是一种基于动量驱动的时序注意力聚合的解码时KV cache压缩方法，通过建模token重要性为持续演化的状态，在保持解码延迟的同时显著提升长文本生成任务的生成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV cache是LLM长文本生成的瓶颈；现有方法对prefill和decoding缓存统一压缩，压缩prefill cache会损害性能；解码阶段压缩方法依赖刚性的近邻窗口或瞬时注意力，无法捕捉token重要性的动态时序模式；静态启发式方法导致过早驱逐重要token或保留陈旧token。

Method: 提出Moment-KV，基于动量驱动的时序注意力聚合的解码时KV cache压缩方法。将token重要性建模为持续演化的状态，注意力以衰减方式聚合，同时捕捉长期影响和近期相关性。

Result: 在长文本生成任务中显著提高生成保真度（2.3-3.2%），同时保持解码延迟。

Conclusion: Moment-KV有效解决了现有KV cache压缩方法在解码阶段的问题，通过动态时序注意力聚合实现了更优的压缩效果，在提升性能的同时不增加延迟开销。

Abstract: Key-Value (KV) cache remains a major bottleneck for deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) in long-generation tasks. Prior work often applies uniform compression across both prefill and decoding caches, but compressing the prefill cache degrades performance by corrupting critical context. While preserving the prefill cache is essential, decoding-phase compression remains underexplored, with existing methods relying on rigid recency windows or instantaneous attention. Our analysis of attention dynamics reveals strong temporal patterns: critical tokens receive sustained attention over long horizons, while local reasoning involves short-lived bursts. Static heuristics fail to capture this behavior, leading to premature eviction of important tokens or retention of stale ones. We propose Moment-KV, a decoding-time KV cache compression method based on momentum-driven temporal attention aggregation. Our method models token importance as a continuously evolving state, where attention is aggregated with decay, capturing both long-term influence and recent relevance. Experiments show that Moment-KV significantly improves generation fidelity in long-generation tasks (2.3-3.2 %) while maintaining decoding latency.

</details>


### [102] [Redundant or Necessary? A Benchmark for Detecting Redundant Steps in Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29893)
*Minyang Hu,Bo Yang,Zhinuo Zhou,Jiachen Liang,Guo Jiahao,Yiyang Yin,Xiongwei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了LLM智能体轨迹中“冗余步骤检测”这一新研究方向，引入RedundancyBench基准测试集，评估现有方法在识别冗余步骤上的表现，结果显示当前方法性能有限，凸显了该任务的研究价值与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评估主要关注任务成功率，忽视了执行效率这一关键方面。实际智能体轨迹常包含贡献极小的冗余步骤，浪费大量资源。本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 1. 将“冗余步骤检测”形式化为一个研究问题；2. 构建RedundancyBench基准测试集，包含多样化任务及人工标注轨迹（每一步均标注其对任务完成的贡献）；3. 在该基准上评估3种代表性检测方法。

Result: 即使表现最佳的方法在检测冗余步骤上的得分仅为24.88%，部分方法甚至低于随机猜测。这表明该任务极具挑战性。

Conclusion: 冗余步骤检测是智能体评估中的重要且被忽视的维度。当前方法能力不足，需要进一步深入研究以提升智能体的执行效率与资源利用率。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in solving complex tasks through multi-step reasoning and tool use. However, existing evaluation protocols primarily focus on task success, overlooking a critical aspect of agent behavior: execution efficiency. In practice, agent trajectories often contain redundant steps that consume substantial resources while contributing little to task completion. In this work, we propose and formulate a new research area: \textbf{redundant step detection} for agent trajectories. To support this initiative, we introduce \textbf{RedundancyBench}, a new benchmark that contains diverse tasks with carefully annotated trajectories, where each step is labeled according to its contribution to task completion. Using RedundancyBench, we develop and evaluate 3 representative methods to answer whether a step within trajectory is redundant or necessary. Our results show that even the best-performing method achieves only 24.88\% score in detecting redundant steps, while some methods perform worse than random guessing. These results highlight the task's complexity and the need for further research in this area. \footnote{Code and dataset in this paper are both available in \href{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RedundancyBench}{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RedundancyBench}.}

</details>


### [103] [Make LLM Learn to Synthesize from Streaming Experiences through Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29940)
*Zhenlin Hu,Yan Wang,Zhen Bi,Zihao Xue,Bingyu Zhu,Longtao Huang,Xiongtao Zhang,Zeyu Yang,Zhixuan Chu,Jungang Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StreamSynth是一种序列合成数据生成的新范式，通过SynLearner框架实现跨任务的经验积累与迁移，使模型能够从历史任务中学习并提升后续合成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将合成数据生成视为孤立任务，忽略了模型能否通过积累历史任务经验并迁移到未来任务这一核心问题。传统方法缺乏对任务流中经验复用机制的研究。

Method: 提出StreamSynth新设置，定义任务流中合成任务的顺序到达模式；设计SynLearner通用框架，鼓励模型探索多样化合成模式、从反馈中学习，并在任务演进中平衡样本质量与集合级多样性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，SynLearner能有效利用前期任务的经验来提升后期任务的合成性能，表现出一致的跨任务可迁移性。

Conclusion: 为StreamSynth范式的可行性提供了证据，将合成数据生成揭示为一种可从任务流中受益的经验驱动过程，为后续研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been widely adopted for synthetic data generation, significantly reducing annotation costs. However, most existing studies treat synthesis as a set of isolated tasks and overlook a more fundamental question: whether a model can learn to synthesize by accumulating experience from past tasks and transferring it to future ones. In this work, we introduce StreamSynth, a new setting in which synthesis tasks arrive sequentially and experience from historical tasks provides informative signals for future synthesis. To address this setting, we propose SynLearner, a general framework that enables synthesis models to acquire reusable synthesis experience over a task stream. Instead of generating data independently for each task, SynLearner encourages the model to explore diverse synthesis patterns, learn from feedback, and balance sample quality with set-level diversity as tasks evolve. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks show that SynLearner effectively leverages experience from earlier tasks to improve synthesis performance on later ones, exhibiting consistent cross-task transferability. These findings provide evidence for the feasibility of StreamSynth and highlight synthetic data generation as an experience-driven process that can benefit from task streams.

</details>


### [104] [From GPS Points to Travel Patterns: Flexible and Semantic Trajectory Generation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30014)
*Silin Zhou,Chenhao Wang,Yuntao Wen,Shuo Shang,Lisi Chen,Panos Kalnis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种名为HTP（层次化旅行模式生成）的方法，通过分层生成旅行模式然后再生成GPS点来克服现有方法在隐私保护、旅行模式捕捉和条件生成方面的局限性。该方法结合了RQ-VAE和LLM，在两个真实数据集上将生成质量提高了29.78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于隐私顾虑，难以获取大规模高质量轨迹数据。现有轨迹生成方法无法明确捕捉旅行模式，且只能在单一条件下生成固定长度的轨迹。因此需要一种既能保护隐私又能灵活生成的替代方案。

Method: 1. 设计轨迹专用残差量化变分自编码器(RQ-VAE)，将微级GPS轨迹量化为紧凑的宏观级旅行模式标记
2. 扩展LLM词汇表，加入旅行模式标记
3. 应用监督微调(SFT)将LLM与轨迹生成任务对齐，实现各种条件下的旅行模式序列生成

Result: 在两个真实数据集上，HTP在生成质量方面超过最强基线29.78%。该方法能够捕捉丰富的段空间不规则性，如由交通状况引起的点密度变化。

Conclusion: HTP成功实现了分层生成旅行模式再生成GPS点的框架，有效解决了隐私顾虑下的轨迹数据获取难题，并在生成质量上取得了显著提升，为智能城市应用提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Urban trajectories play a crucial role in modeling urban dynamics and supporting various smart city applications. However, privacy concerns restrict access to large-scale and high-quality trajectory datasets. Trajectory generation provides a promising alternative by synthesizing realistic data to mitigate privacy risks. However, existing methods fail to explicitly capture travel patterns and can only generate fixed-length trajectories under a single condition. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{HTP}, which \textbf{H}ierarchically generates \textbf{T}ravel patterns first and then generates GPS \textbf{P}oints by using large language models (LLMs), rather than directly generating GPS points. We first design a trajectory-specific residual quantization variational autoencoder (RQ-VAE) that quantizes micro-level GPS trajectories into compact, macro-level travel pattern tokens in a coarse-to-fine manner. These tokens capture rich segment spatial irregularities, such as point density variations caused by traffic conditions. Then, we extend the LLM vocabulary with travel pattern tokens to align trajectory representations with the LLM input, and apply supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to align the LLM with the trajectory generation task, enabling generation of travel pattern sequences under various conditions. Extensive experiments on two real-world datasets show that HTP outperforms the strongest baseline by an average of 29.78\% in terms of generation quality. Our code is available at https://github.com/slzhou-xy/HTP.

</details>


### [105] [Domain-Specific Data Synthesis for LLMs via Minimal Sufficient Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30039)
*Tong Ye,Hang Yu,Tengfei Ma,Xuhong Zhang,Jianguo Li,Peng Di,Peiyu Liu,Jianwei Yin,Wenhai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DOMINO的新框架，通过归纳范式从参考样本中学习最小充分领域表示，并利用对比解耦目标生成领域对齐的合成数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据合成方法采用演绎范式，严重依赖显式的自然语言领域描述和精心设计的提示工程，限制了其在难以描述或形式化表达的领域中的适用性；获取目标领域的高质量数据仍然是一个重大挑战。

Method: DOMINO框架通过提示微调结合对比解耦目标，从参考样本中学习最小充分领域表示，将领域级模式与样本特定噪声分离，在缓解过拟合的同时保留核心领域特征。

Result: 在领域定义隐含的挑战性编程基准上，使用DOMINO合成的数据进行微调，Pass@1准确率相比强大的指令调优基座模型提高了最多4.63%，证明了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 这项工作为领域特定数据合成建立了新范式，能够在无需手动提示设计或自然语言领域规范的情况下实现实用且可扩展的领域适应。

Abstract: Large Language Models have demonstrated remarkable progress in general-purpose capabilities and can achieve strong performance in specific domains through fine-tuning on domain-specific data. However, acquiring high-quality data for target domains remains a significant challenge. Existing data synthesis approaches follow a deductive paradigm, heavily relying on explicit domain descriptions expressed in natural language and careful prompt engineering, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios where domains are difficult to describe or formally articulate. In this work, we tackle the underexplored problem of domain-specific data synthesis through an inductive paradigm, where the target domain is defined only through a set of reference examples, particularly when domain characteristics are difficult to articulate in natural language. We propose a novel framework, DOMINO, that learns a minimal sufficient domain representation from reference samples and leverages it to guide the generation of domain-aligned synthetic data. DOMINO integrates prompt tuning with a contrastive disentanglement objective to separate domain-level patterns from sample-specific noise, mitigating overfitting while preserving core domain characteristics. Theoretically, we prove that DOMINO expands the support of the synthetic data distribution, ensuring greater diversity. Empirically, on challenging coding benchmarks where domain definitions are implicit, fine-tuning on data synthesized by DOMINO improves Pass@1 accuracy by up to 4.63\% over strong, instruction-tuned backbones, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness. This work establishes a new paradigm for domain-specific data synthesis, enabling practical and scalable domain adaptation without manual prompt design or natural language domain specifications.

</details>


### [106] [Learning to Choose: An Empowerment-Guided Multi-Agent System with semantic communication for Adaptive Method Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30042)
*Geremy Loachamín-Suntaxi,Robert Lazar,Dimitrios G. Giovanis,Ioannis G. Kevrekidis,Eleni D. Koronaki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合上下文_bandits、结构化通信和语义检查点的多智能体框架，通过保持动作-结果一致性来防止科学计算工作流中的语义漂移，在敏感性分析和不确定性量化任务中显著提升了策略学习的收敛性、鲁棒性和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化科学计算工作流不仅需要生成可执行代码，还需要智能体选择适当计算策略、忠实实现并确保因果归因。在多智能体管道中，意图与行动之间的小不一致会导致语义漂移，使执行过程不再反映原始策略，从而破坏下游评估和自适应能力。

Method: 采用多智能体框架，整合：1) 上下文_bandits进行自适应决策；2) 结构化智能体间通信；3) 语义检查点确保动作-结果保真度；4) 专用大语言模型智能体；5) 基于代码的生成；6) 自愈执行循环。以赋能视角分析，强调可靠自主学习需保证高质动作的传播完整性。

Result: 通过敏感性分析和不确定性量化工作流验证：不受控的语义漂移会降低策略学习性能；提出的框架显著提升收敛速度、鲁棒性和对新颖问题情境的适应能力。

Conclusion: 科学多智能体系统应遵循更广泛的设计原则：自适应决策必须与显式机制耦合，以确保计算管道中的语义一致性和可靠信息流。

Abstract: Automating scientific computing workflows requires more than generating executable code: autonomous systems must also select appropriate computational strategies, implement them faithfully, and ensure that the resulting outcomes remain causally attributable to the decisions that produced them. In multi-agent pipelines, this process is particularly fragile, as small inconsistencies between agent intentions and actions can lead to semantic drift, where the eventually executed procedure no longer reflects the originally selected strategy, thereby corrupting downstream evaluation and adaptation. In this work, motivated by the ATHENA framework (Toscano et al., 2025; Toscano et al., 2026) and the concept of empowerment (Yiu et al., 2025), we introduce a multi-agent framework that combines contextual bandits with structured inter-agent communication and, most importantly, semantic checkpoints that preserve action-outcome fidelity throughout the pipeline. The system integrates specialized large language model (LLM) agents, grounded code generation, and self-healing execution loops within an adaptive decision-making architecture. Interpreting the framework through the lens of empowerment, we show that reliable autonomous learning requires not only identifying high-quality actions, but also preserving the integrity of their propagation across agents. Using sensitivity analysis and uncertainty quantification workflows as representative case studies, we demonstrate that unchecked semantic drift degrades policy learning, whereas the proposed framework improves convergence, robustness, and adaptation to novel problem contexts. These results suggest a broader design principle for scientific multi-agent systems: adaptive decision-making must be coupled with explicit mechanisms that guarantee semantic consistency and reliable information flow across the computational pipeline.

</details>


### [107] [Modularizing Educational LLM-Agency for Fostering Responsible Learning Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30187)
*Julius Gabelmann,Felix Jahn,Kevin Baum,Sophie van Rossum,Emely Wuenscher,Timo P. Gros,Verena Wolf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种模块化智能体AI聊天机器人架构，用于辅助学生解题，旨在通过融入教学指导而非直接给出答案，提升教育中AI使用的责任性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI聊天机器人在教育中的广泛应用引发了责任部署的担忧。大型语言模型（LLM）虽然可能访问教育科学文献，但缺乏对教学概念的遵循倾向，可能导致学习过程出现负面效果，如削弱迁移能力、批判性思维和创造力。

Method: 基于对负责任LLM教育系统的需求识别，论证了单体式、开箱即用解决方案的结构缺陷，提出模块化智能体架构。针对解题的不同阶段设计具体模块，以融入有针对性的教学建议。

Result: 构建了可集成教学指导的模块化智能体架构，能够在解题过程中提供更可控、透明和可监督的指导，而非直接提供答案。

Conclusion: 模块化智能体架构能够增强教育中AI使用的责任性，通过在解题各阶段融入教学指导，促进学生的批判性思维和创造力发展。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of AI chatbots in education will drastically change learning, making responsible deployment a critical concern. While large language models (LLMs) might have access to sources discussing insights from educational sciences, they are not particularly inclined to adhere to pedagogical concepts, risking negative effects on the learning process, such as a loss of transfer capabilities, critical thinking, or creativity. In this paper, we introduce an agentic AI chatbot architecture assisting students with exercise solving, specifically designed to contribute to more responsible AI use in education. We base our conceptual development on the identification of several desiderata for responsible LLM-based educational systems, argue for the structural shortcomings inherent in monolithic, out-of-the-box solutions, and instead suggest modularizing the agentic architecture. We propose specific modules for different stages of exercise solving, enabling incorporation of targeted pedagogical advice, guiding students through the learning process in a more controllable, transparent, and overseeable manner.

</details>


### [108] [When Should Models Change Their Minds? Contextual Belief Management in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30219)
*Haoming Xu,Weihong Xu,Zongrui Li,Mengru Wang,Yunzhi Yao,Chiyu Wu,Jin Shang,Yu Gong,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出“情境信念管理（CBM）”框架以解决语言模型在长时交互中管理累积信息的挑战，设计了BeliefTrack基准测试，发现原始模型存在严重CBM失败，而强化学习和表示层引导可分别降低70.9%和46.1%的失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时交互要求语言模型管理累积信息，决定何时更新、保留或忽略状态，将此挑战定义为情境信念管理（CBM）。

Method: 提出CBM框架；创建BeliefTrack基准测试（Rule Discovery和Circuit Diagnosis任务，有限信念空间与符号验证器）；测试三种失败模式（Failed Stay、Failed Update、Failed Isolation）；比较原始模型、显式信念跟踪提示、强化学习（信念状态奖励）及表示层引导。

Result: 原始模型存在严重CBM失败；显式提示增益有限；RL将平均失败率降低70.9%；表示层引导将失败率降低46.1%（两任务）。

Conclusion: CBM是LLM的重要挑战，RL与表示层引导能有效缓解CBM失败，提供了代码实现（https://github.com/zjunlp/CBM）。

Abstract: Long-horizon interactions require language models to manage accumulating information: when to update their state, when to preserve their state, and what to ignore. We study this challenge as \textbf{Contextual Belief Management (CBM)}: maintaining a predicted belief state aligned with formal evidence while isolating task-irrelevant noise. To make CBM measurable, we introduce BeliefTrack, a closed-world benchmark spanning Rule Discovery and Circuit Diagnosis, where a finite belief space and symbolic verifiers enable exact turn-level evaluation. BeliefTrack diagnoses three failures: Failed Stay, Failed Update, and Failed Isolation. Across multiple LLMs, vanilla models exhibit severe CBM failures, while explicit belief-tracking prompts provide limited gains. In contrast, reinforcement learning with belief-state rewards reduces failure rates by 70.9\% on average. Further probing reveals latent belief-state dynamics behind these failures, and representation-level steering reduces failure rates by 46.1\% across two tasks\footnote{Code is coming soon at https://github.com/zjunlp/CBM.

</details>


### [109] [ProjectionBench: Evaluating Scientific Hypothesis Generation in LLMs Under Progressive Information Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30284)
*A. J. Lew,Y. Cao,M. J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个渐进式信息披露的科学发现与推理基准框架，评估LLM在从假设生成到零假设检验的全过程中处理科学问题的能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现需要超越已知知识检索的创新推理能力，但现有基于多跳检索的基准测试未能充分评估LLM的这一核心能力

Method: 构建从原始问题到零假设检验的渐进式框架，模型在逐步揭示技术细节的过程中生成假设，通过原子主张的自动化语义相似性比较评估与真实结论的偏离程度

Result: 在45篇材料科学论文上评估GPT-5、GPT-5.4、Gemini 2.5 Pro和Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview，发现GPT-5.4和Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview显著优于前代模型，GPT-5.4在最小上下文下仍保持0.7 F1分数与真实结论对齐

Conclusion: 该框架为系统评估LLM的科学推理和发现能力提供了基础，是发展下一代AI科学家/合作科学家系统的关键步骤

Abstract: Scientific discovery is an inherently creative and uncertain process, requiring reasoning beyond the recall of known knowledge. While many benchmarks have been proposed to evaluate large language model (LLM) performance on deep research tasks via multi-hop retrieval, their innovative reasoning abilities essential for true scientific discovery remain largely untested. We introduce a benchmark framework for evaluating model performance in scientific discovery and reasoning, building up from a raw problem to the classical null hypothesis test. In our framework, models initially receive only the topic and research question from a recent paper, with technical details progressively revealed. At each stage of information disclosure, the model is tasked with generating hypotheses that address the research question, which is compared with the conclusions from the original paper and evaluated via automated semantic similarity of constituent atomic claims. This progressive evaluation of semantic divergence from ground-truth conclusions enables assessment of a model's innovativeness (under minimal information) to grounded reasoning capabilities (under full experimental details), both critical for using LLMs for scientific discovery purposes. Our framework provides a foundation for systematically evaluating scientific reasoning and discovery capabilities in LLMs, crucial for advancing the development of next-generation AI scientist/co-scientist systems. Specifically, here we evaluate GPT-5, GPT-5.4, Gemini 2.5 pro, and Gemini 3.1 pro preview across 45 papers spanning bioactive materials, mechanical materials, and nanomaterials. We find that GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3.1 pro outperform their previous generation counterparts as expected, and GPT-5.4 in particular maintains 0.7 F1 score alignment with ground truth conclusions even under minimal context.

</details>


### [110] [Demystifying Data Organization for Enhanced LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30334)
*Yalun Dai,Yangyu Huang,Tongshen Yang,Yonghan Wang,Xin Zhang,Wenshan Wu,Qihao Zhao,Hao Li,Yuanyuan Gao,Kim-Hui Yap,Scarlett Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探索了大语言模型训练中数据组织策略对训练效率和性能的影响，提出四种数据组织准则和两种新型数据排序方法（STR和SAW），在预训练和SFT阶段均显著提升了训练稳定性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM训练效率高度依赖数据编排，虽然数据选择已有大量研究，但数据组织策略仍被忽视，特别是当LLM仅训练1-2个epoch时，数据顺序对训练效果影响显著。

Method: 利用预先计算样本级评分（数据效率相关），在极小额外计算开销下，系统探索数据组织对LLM训练的影响。提出四大优化准则：边界锐化、循环调度、课程连续性和局部多样性，并引入STR和SAW两种新型数据排序方法。

Result: 在多种模型规模和数据规模下验证了方法有效性，涵盖预训练和SFT阶段，证明所提准则和排序方法能显著提升LLM训练的稳定性和性能，且具备良好鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的四种数据组织准则及STR、SAW方法可有效优化LLM训练过程，在保持低计算开销的同时显著提升训练效率和最终模型性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized various fields, yet their training efficiency is heavily reliant on effective data curation. While data selection has been widely studied, the strategic data organization for enhanced training remains an underexplored area, particularly since current LLMs are often trained for only one or a few epochs. This paper systematically explores the influence of data organization on LLM training by reusing pre-computed sample-level scores originally generated for data efficiency, thereby incurring minimal additional computational overhead.
